def _fixture_module():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec("app.workflow_fixture_executor") is not None, (
        "fixture executor module is missing"
    )
    return importlib.import_module("app.workflow_fixture_executor")


def _convert_to_incremental_run(run: ProductionRun) -> dict:
    """Give a real-model fixture the frozen identity required by autorun."""

    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    runtime = snapshot["runtime_context"]
    runtime.pop("baseline_run_id", None)
    runtime.pop("baseline_item_id", None)
    runtime.update(
        {
            "run_mode": "incremental",
            "queue_class": "production_batch",
            "content_ingress_event_db_id": 7001,
            "real_execution_authority": "frozen_incremental_autorun_policy",
            "incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash": "a" * 64,
            "content_ingress_payload_hash": "b" * 64,
            "upstream_intake_payload_hash": "c" * 64,
        }
    )
    snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["run_modes"] = ["incremental"]
    encoded = canonical_json(snapshot)
    run.source_type = "content_ingress_event"
    run.source_id = 7001
    run.queue_class = "production_batch"
    run.snapshot_json = encoded
    run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(encoded.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return snapshot


def _latest_inspiration_single_contract() -> dict:
    evaluation_contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    evaluation_contract["image_defects_enabled"] = False
    evaluation_contract["aesthetic_foundation"]["dimension_keys"] = list(
        LATEST_INSPIRATION_SEMANTIC_DIMENSION_KEYS
    )
    return merged_strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        ["A", "B"],
        evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
        name="inspiration_latest_single",
        description="灵感图最新 A/B 职责单调用严格输出",
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )

def test_identity_transform_and_noop_are_deterministic() -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    identity = fixture.execute_fixture(
        "identity",
        {"content_key": "3d:1:42", "title": "A"},
        {"fixture": "identity"},
        attempt_no=1,
    )
    assert identity.output_manifest["content_key"] == "3d:1:42"

    transformed = fixture.execute_fixture(
        "transform",
        {"content_key": "3d:1:42", "title": "A"},
        {"fixture": "transform", "mapping": {"asset_key": "content_key"}},
        attempt_no=1,
    )
    assert transformed.output_manifest == {"asset_key": "3d:1:42"}

    noop = fixture.execute_fixture(
        "noop",
        {"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
        {"fixture": "noop"},
        attempt_no=1,
    )
    assert noop.output_manifest["fixture"] == "noop"
    assert noop.output_hash == fixture.hash_manifest(noop.output_manifest)


def test_fail_once_has_no_external_execution_surface() -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    try:
        fixture.execute_fixture(
            "fail_once",
            {"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
            {"fixture": "fail_once", "source": "print('unsafe')"},
            attempt_no=1,
        )
    except fixture.FixtureExecutionError as exc:
        assert exc.code == "arbitrary_code_field_forbidden"
    else:
        raise AssertionError("unsafe fixture manifest was accepted")

    try:
        fixture.execute_fixture(
            "fail_once",
            {"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
            {"fixture": "fail_once"},
            attempt_no=1,
        )
    except fixture.FixtureExecutionError as exc:
        assert exc.code == "FIXTURE_FAIL_ONCE"
    else:
        raise AssertionError("fail_once did not fail its first attempt")

    success = fixture.execute_fixture(
        "fail_once",
        {"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
        {"fixture": "fail_once"},
        attempt_no=2,
    )
    assert success.output_manifest["fixture"] == "fail_once"


def test_runtime_failure_classifier_owns_fixture_retry_policy() -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as failure_info:
        fixture.execute_fixture(
            "fail_once",
            {"asset_id": 1},
            {"fixture": "fail_once"},
            attempt_no=1,
        )
    transient = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        failure_info.value,
        error_code=failure_info.value.code,
    )
    assert transient.classification == "transient"
    assert transient.retryable is True

    contract = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        fixture.FixtureExecutionError(
            "exclusive_route_overlap",
            "同一素材同时命中两条互斥路线",
        ),
        error_code="exclusive_route_overlap",
    )
    assert contract.classification == "contract"
    assert contract.retryable is False

    unknown = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        fixture.FixtureExecutionError(
            "model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
            "供应商结果未知",
            retryable=True,
        ),
        error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
    )
    assert unknown.classification == "unknown_external_state"
    assert unknown.retryable is False


def test_workflow_v3_nodes_map_to_fixture_steps_by_instance_id() -> None:
    from app.workflow_composer_runtime import composer_runtime_steps

    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_c_instance",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "redline_after_prompt",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "foreach_case_images",
                "kind": "foreach_image",
                "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "aggregate_case",
                "kind": "aggregate_case",
                "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            },
        ],
    }

    steps = composer_runtime_steps(manifest)

    assert [(step["key"], step["type"]) for step in steps] == [
        ("prompt_c_instance", "model_call"),
        ("redline_after_prompt", "rule_eval"),
        ("foreach_case_images", "model_call"),
        ("aggregate_case", "reconcile"),
    ]
    assert steps[1]["script_version"] == "fixture.composer.rule_module@1"


def test_aggregate_case_rejects_output_outside_its_frozen_schema() -> None:
    """Catches runtime-managed aggregate output bypassing node validation."""

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    node = {
        "node_instance_id": "aggregate_case",
        "kind": "aggregate_case",
        "config": {
            "foreach_node_id": "foreach_images",
            "included_statuses": ["succeeded"],
            "score_path": "$.score",
            "feature_paths": {},
            "empty_policy": "fail",
            "failure_projection": "include",
        },
        "output_schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"impossible": {"type": "string"}},
            "required": ["impossible"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
    }
    input_manifest = {
        "upstream": {
            "foreach_images": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-foreach-output-v1",
                "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
                "source_hash": "a" * 64,
                "item_contract_hash": "b" * 64,
                "counts": {
                    "total": 1,
                    "succeeded": 1,
                    "failed": 0,
                    "skipped": 0,
                },
                "items": [
                    {
                        "item_key": "image-a",
                        "ordinal": 0,
                        "status": "succeeded",
                        "attempt_count": 1,
                        "input_hash": "c" * 64,
                        "output_hash": "d" * 64,
                        "output": {"score": 88},
                    }
                ],
            }
        }
    }

    with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
        fixture._aggregate_case_result(
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            input_fingerprint="e" * 64,
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "workflow_node_output_schema_invalid"


def test_workflow_v3_knowledge_graph_prompt_emits_the_frozen_dry_run_shape() -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(
        "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"
    )
    assert contract is not None
    run_snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_c_instance",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "config": {
                            "prompt_version_id": 11,
                            "output_contract_key": contract.contract_key,
                            "output_contract_snapshot": contract.snapshot(),
                            "component_snapshot": {
                                "content_hash": "a" * 64,
                            },
                        },
                        "output_schema": contract.output_schema,
                        "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "ll_id"],
                    }
                ],
            }
        }
    }

    result = fixture._composer_v3_output(
        run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
        step_key="prompt_c_instance",
        input_manifest={"request_id": "req-7", "payload": {"ll_id": "asset-42"}},
        input_hash="b" * 64,
        raw_output={"fixture": "noop"},
        attempt_no=1,
        started_at=datetime(2026, 8, 26, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    assert result is not None
    assert list(result["output"]) == list(contract.field_order)
    assert result["output"]["ll_id"] == "asset-42"
    assert result["output"]["is_single"] == 0
    assert all(
        result["output"][field] == ""
        for field in contract.field_order
        if field not in {"ll_id", "is_single"}
    )
    assert result["output_contract_key"] == contract.contract_key
    assert result["output_contract_hash"] == contract.snapshot()["contract_hash"]
    assert result["evidence"] == {"request_id": "req-7", "ll_id": "asset-42"}

        "kg.model_3d_su.overseas.single.v1"
    )
    assert contract is not None
