        "workflow": {
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_overseas",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "config": {
                            "output_contract_snapshot": contract.snapshot(),
                            "component_snapshot": {},
                        },
                        "output_schema": contract.output_schema,
                        "evidence_fields": [],
                    }
                ],
            }
        }
    }

    try:
        fixture._composer_v3_output(
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
            step_key="prompt_overseas",
            input_manifest={"ll_id": "domestic-only"},
            input_hash="c" * 64,
            raw_output={"fixture": "noop"},
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )
    except fixture.FixtureExecutionError as exc:
        assert exc.code == "kg_fixture_identity_missing"
    else:
        raise AssertionError("knowledge-graph fixture accepted a missing route identity")


def test_real_prompt_provider_timeout_is_unknown_and_never_retryable(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """A read timeout may follow provider execution, so it must never resend."""

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-timeout.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class TimeoutClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                pass

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_options):
                raise DoubaoTransportError("timeout")

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=TimeoutClient,
            )

        assert exc_info.value.code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
        assert exc_info.value.retryable is False
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            assert json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)["failure"] == {
                "attempt_count": 0,
                "retryable": False,
                "technical_error_type": "timeout",
                        "detail": "模型 API timeout",
        }
            ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
            assert ledger is not None
            assert ledger.request_count == 1
            assert ledger.reserved_input_tokens == 12_000
            assert ledger.reserved_output_tokens == 512
            assert ledger.estimated_cost_micros == 13_024
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_uses_renewable_worker_lease_independent_of_provider_timeout(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-long-lease.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    heartbeat_leases: list[int] = []
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            model.timeout_seconds = 900
            node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["runtime_config"][
                "timeout_seconds"
            ] = 900
            model_snapshot = dict(node["config"]["model_snapshot"])
            model_snapshot.pop("snapshot_hash", None)
            node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["snapshot_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(model_snapshot).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0] = node
            encoded = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(encoded.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        original_heartbeat = fixture.heartbeat_runtime_step

        def record_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs):
            heartbeat_leases.append(int(kwargs.get("lease_seconds", 300)))
            return original_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs)

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "heartbeat_runtime_step", record_heartbeat)

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "通过"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-long-lease"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-long-lease",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        fixture._real_prompt_result(
            worker_id="worker-1",
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            model_client_type=SuccessClient,
            complete_parent=False,
        )

        assert heartbeat_leases[0] == fixture.DEFAULT_LEASE_SECONDS
        assert heartbeat_leases[0] >= 3 * fixture._PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_heartbeats_parent_while_provider_is_still_running(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    import asyncio
    import threading

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-periodic-heartbeat.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    heartbeat_threads: list[str] = []
    provider_active = threading.Event()
    provider_heartbeat_seen = threading.Event()
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS",
            0.01,
        )
        original_heartbeat = fixture.heartbeat_runtime_step

        def record_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs):
            if provider_active.is_set():
                heartbeat_threads.append(threading.current_thread().name)
                provider_heartbeat_seen.set()
            return original_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs)

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "heartbeat_runtime_step", record_heartbeat)

        class SlowSuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                provider_active.set()
                assert await asyncio.to_thread(
                    provider_heartbeat_seen.wait,
                    1.0,
                )
                provider_active.clear()
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "通过"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-periodic-heartbeat"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-periodic-heartbeat",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        fixture._real_prompt_result(
            worker_id="worker-1",
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            model_client_type=SlowSuccessClient,
            complete_parent=False,
        )

        assert heartbeat_threads
        assert threading.current_thread().name not in heartbeat_threads

    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_client_preflight_failure_is_safe_to_retry(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Credential/client construction failure happens before provider dispatch."""

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-preflight.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class UnavailablePreflightClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                raise RuntimeError("credential store temporarily unavailable")

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=UnavailablePreflightClient,
            )

        assert exc_info.value.code == "model_execution_preflight_failed"
        assert exc_info.value.retryable is True
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "rejected"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_execution_preflight_failed"
            evidence = json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)
            assert evidence["daily_budget_release"]["reason"] == (
                "provider_not_dispatched"
            )
            ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
            assert ledger is not None
            assert ledger.request_count == 0
            assert ledger.reserved_input_tokens == 0
            assert ledger.reserved_output_tokens == 0
            assert ledger.estimated_cost_micros == 0
            fixture.fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=invocation.id,
                error_code="model_execution_preflight_failed",
                error_message=(
                    "模型请求尚未发出，前置准备暂时失败，可按节点策略重试"
                ),
                evidence={
                    "attempt_count": 0,
                    "retryable": True,
                    "technical_error_type": "RuntimeError",
                },
                provider_dispatched=False,
            )
            db.commit()
            assert ledger.request_count == 0
            assert ledger.reserved_input_tokens == 0
            assert ledger.reserved_output_tokens == 0
            assert ledger.estimated_cost_micros == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _assert_concurrent_preflight_rejection_is_idempotent(
    engine,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
            db,
            tmp_path,
            monkeypatch,
        )
        run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
        invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
            db,
            attempt_id=attempt.id,
            node=node,
            run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
        )
        ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
        assert ledger is not None
        # Keep unrelated reservations in the shared ledger.  Without an
        # invocation-row lock, two identical rejectors can both refund this
        # invocation and the underflow guard cannot detect the second refund.
        unrelated_count = 3
        ledger.request_count += unrelated_count
        ledger.reserved_input_tokens += (
            unrelated_count * invocation.reserved_input_tokens
        )
        ledger.reserved_output_tokens += (
            unrelated_count * invocation.reserved_output_tokens
        )
        ledger.estimated_cost_micros += (
            unrelated_count * invocation.estimated_cost_micros
        )
        invocation_id = invocation.id
        reserved_input = invocation.reserved_input_tokens
        reserved_output = invocation.reserved_output_tokens
        reserved_cost = invocation.estimated_cost_micros
        db.commit()

    start = Barrier(2)

    def reject_once() -> str:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            start.wait(timeout=5)
            row = fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=invocation_id,
                error_code="model_execution_preflight_failed",
                error_message="provider was not dispatched",
                evidence={"retryable": True, "attempt_count": 0},
                provider_dispatched=False,
            )
            db.commit()
            return row.status

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
        outcomes = list(executor.map(lambda _index: reject_once(), range(2)))

    assert outcomes == ["rejected", "rejected"]
    with Session(engine) as db:
        invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
        ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
        assert invocation is not None and invocation.status == "rejected"
        assert ledger is not None
        assert ledger.request_count == unrelated_count
        assert ledger.reserved_input_tokens == unrelated_count * reserved_input
        assert ledger.reserved_output_tokens == unrelated_count * reserved_output
        assert ledger.estimated_cost_micros == unrelated_count * reserved_cost


def test_mysql_concurrent_preflight_rejection_refunds_once(
    mysql_engine,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    _assert_concurrent_preflight_rejection_is_idempotent(
        mysql_engine,
        tmp_path,
        monkeypatch,
    )


def test_postgres_concurrent_preflight_rejection_refunds_once(
    pg_engine,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
    _assert_concurrent_preflight_rejection_is_idempotent(
        pg_engine,
        tmp_path,
        monkeypatch,
    )


def _assert_concurrent_settlement_has_one_authoritative_result(
    engine,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
            db,
            tmp_path,
            monkeypatch,
        )
        run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
        invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
            db,
            attempt_id=attempt.id,
            node=node,
            run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
        )
        invocation_id = invocation.id
        db.commit()

    start = Barrier(2)

    def settle_once(index: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            start.wait(timeout=5)
            try:
                row = settle_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=invocation_id,
                    request_correlation_id=f"request-{index}",
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    input_tokens=100 + index,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    evidence={"result_index": index},
                )
                db.commit()
                return row.status, index
            except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
                db.rollback()
                return exc.code, index

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
        outcomes = list(executor.map(settle_once, range(2)))

    assert sorted(status for status, _index in outcomes) == [
        "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict",
        "succeeded",
    ]
    winner = next(index for status, index in outcomes if status == "succeeded")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
        assert invocation is not None
        assert invocation.status == "succeeded"
        assert invocation.request_correlation_id == f"request-{winner}"
        assert invocation.input_tokens == 100 + winner
        assert json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)["result"] == {
            "result_index": winner
        }


def test_mysql_concurrent_settlement_keeps_one_authoritative_result(
    mysql_engine,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    _assert_concurrent_settlement_has_one_authoritative_result(
        mysql_engine,
        tmp_path,
        monkeypatch,
    )


def test_postgres_concurrent_settlement_keeps_one_authoritative_result(
    pg_engine,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
    _assert_concurrent_settlement_has_one_authoritative_result(
        pg_engine,
        tmp_path,
        monkeypatch,
    )


def test_media_preparation_failure_stops_before_provider_and_ledger_reservation(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'media-preflight.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            run_snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        def fail_media_preflight(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise fixture.WorkflowModelExecutorError(
                "image_payload_too_large_after_lossless",
                "图片仍超过供应商安全上限",
                retryable=False,
                evidence={
                    "sent_bytes": 101,
                    "operational_max_bytes": 100,
                },
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "prepare_prompt_invocation",
            fail_media_preflight,
        )

        class ProviderMustNotRun:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                raise AssertionError("provider client must not be constructed")

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=ProviderMustNotRun,
            )

        assert exc_info.value.code == "image_payload_too_large_after_lossless"
        assert exc_info.value.retryable is False
        assert exc_info.value.evidence == {
            "sent_bytes": 101,
            "operational_max_bytes": 100,
        }
        with Session(engine) as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_invalid_frozen_short_circuit_stops_before_provider_and_reservation(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'short-circuit-preflight.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    provider_constructions = 0
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            invalid_rules = [
                {
                    "rule_version": "short-circuit-rule-v1",
                    "rule_key": "invalid-in-operand",
                    "when": {
                        "path": "output.level",
                        "op": "in",
                        "value": "L4",
                    },
                    "target_level": "L4",
                    "priority": 50,
                    "skip_roles": ["grading"],
                }
            ]
            node["config"]["short_circuit_rules"] = invalid_rules
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "short_circuit_rules"
            ] = invalid_rules
            encoded = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(encoded.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class ProviderMustNotRun:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                nonlocal provider_constructions
                provider_constructions += 1

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=ProviderMustNotRun,
            )

        assert exc_info.value.code == "short_circuit_condition_invalid"
        assert provider_constructions == 0
        with Session(engine) as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure_phase", ["heartbeat", "settlement"])
def test_post_provider_database_failure_never_resends_paid_request(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    failure_phase: str,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'post-provider-{failure_phase}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    provider_calls = 0
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            run_snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                nonlocal provider_calls
                provider_calls += 1
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "通过"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-post-provider"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-post-provider",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        database_error = OperationalError(
            "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations",
            {},
            sqlite3.OperationalError("database is locked"),
        )
        if failure_phase == "heartbeat":
            original_heartbeat = fixture.heartbeat_runtime_step
            heartbeat_calls = 0

            def fail_second_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs):
                nonlocal heartbeat_calls
                heartbeat_calls += 1
                if heartbeat_calls == 2:
                    raise database_error
                return original_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs)

            monkeypatch.setattr(
                fixture,
                "heartbeat_runtime_step",
                fail_second_heartbeat,
            )
        else:
            monkeypatch.setattr(
                fixture,
                "settle_model_invocation",
                lambda *_args, **_kwargs: (_ for _ in ()).throw(database_error),
            )

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=SuccessClient,
            )

        assert provider_calls == 1
        assert exc_info.value.code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
        assert exc_info.value.retryable is False
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("conflicts", [1, 4])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure_stage", ["settlement", "completion"])
def test_known_provider_result_retries_only_settlement_transaction(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path, conflicts, failure_stage,
):
    """A rolled-back DB conflict must not discard usage or resend the provider."""
    from pymysql.err import OperationalError as MysqlError

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'settlement-conflict.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    provider_calls = 0
    settlement_sessions = []
    original_settle = fixture.settle_model_invocation
    original_complete = fixture.complete_runtime_step
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
            # This single-node fixture has no final baseline projection owner.
            run.total_steps = 2
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            inputs = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id, token = attempt.id, attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                nonlocal provider_calls
                provider_calls += 1
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "通过"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-db-conflict"},
                    upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id="req-db-conflict",
                    attempt_count=1, output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100,
                )

        def conflict_after_writes(db, *args, **kwargs):
            settlement_sessions.append(db)
            operation = original_settle if failure_stage == "settlement" else original_complete
            result = operation(db, *args, **kwargs)
            if len(settlement_sessions) <= conflicts:
                # Exercise actual rollback of settled ledger/media changes.
                raise OperationalError("UPDATE workflow_model_invocations", {},
                                       MysqlError(1213, "controlled deadlock"))
            return result

        target = "settle_model_invocation" if failure_stage == "settlement" else "complete_runtime_step"
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, target, conflict_after_writes)
        arguments = dict(worker_id="worker-1", attempt_id=attempt_id,
                         lease_token=token, run_snapshot=snapshot, node=node,
                         input_manifest=inputs, model_client_type=SuccessClient)
        if conflicts == 4:
            with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as error:
                fixture._real_prompt_result(**arguments)
            assert error.value.code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            assert error.value.retryable is False
        else:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(**arguments)
        assert provider_calls == 1
        assert len(settlement_sessions) == (2 if conflicts == 1 else 4)
        assert len({id(db) for db in settlement_sessions}) == len(settlement_sessions)
        with sessions() as db:
            rows = list(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)))
            assert len(rows) == 1
            assert rows[0].status == ("succeeded" if conflicts == 1 else "failed")
            if conflicts == 1:
                assert (rows[0].input_tokens, rows[0].output_tokens) == (80, 20)
                assert rows[0].request_correlation_id == "req-db-conflict"
                assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_settlement_completes_runtime_attempt_in_same_operation(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-atomic-completion.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            run.total_steps = 2
            run_snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            run_id = run.id
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "通过"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-atomic-safe"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-atomic-safe",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        result = fixture._real_prompt_result(
            worker_id="worker-1",
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
            node=node,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            model_client_type=SuccessClient,
        )

        with sessions() as db:
            persisted_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            persisted_run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted_attempt is not None
            assert persisted_attempt.status == "succeeded"
            assert json.loads(persisted_attempt.output_manifest_json) == (
                result.output_manifest
            )
            assert invocation is not None and invocation.status == "succeeded"
            assert persisted_run is not None and persisted_run.completed_steps == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("second_inconsistent", "expected_run_status"),
    ((False, "succeeded"), (True, "failed")),
)
def test_inspiration_semantic_retry_runs_once_through_real_worker(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    second_inconsistent: bool,
    expected_run_status: str,
) -> None:
    """Worker retries one settled conflict and never creates attempt 3."""

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'semantic-worker-{int(second_inconsistent)}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    responses = [True, second_inconsistent]
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            contract = _latest_inspiration_single_contract()
            node["config"]["output_contract"] = contract
            node["output_schema"] = deepcopy(contract["schema"])
            node["retry_policy"] = {
                "kind": "fixed",
                "max_attempts": 2,
                "delay_seconds": 0,
            }
            attempt.script_version.max_attempts = 2
            attempt.script_version.retry_policy_json = canonical_json(
                node["retry_policy"]
            )
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            run_snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_budget"].update(
                {
                    "max_requests": 2,
                    "max_input_tokens": 30_000,
                    "max_output_tokens": 2_048,
                    "max_cost_micros": 100_000,
                }
            )
            manifest = run_snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]
            manifest["nodes"] = [node]
            manifest["resource_policy"] = {
                "source_provenance": {
                    "contract_profile": LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
                }
            }
            encoded_snapshot = canonical_json(run_snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded_snapshot
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                encoded_snapshot.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            run.status = "retryable"
            run.lease_owner = None
            run.lease_token = None
            run.lease_expires_at = None
            attempt.status = "retryable"
            attempt.lease_owner = None
            attempt.lease_token = None
            attempt.lease_expires_at = None
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            assert dispatch is not None
            dispatch.status = "queued"
            dispatch.lease_owner = None
            dispatch.lease_token = None
            dispatch.leased_at = None
            dispatch.lease_expires_at = None
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SequentialClient:
            calls = 0

            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                inconsistent = responses[self.__class__.calls]
                self.__class__.calls += 1
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=_latest_inspiration_single_payload(
                        inconsistent=inconsistent
                    ),
                    raw_text="{}",
                    raw_payload={
                        "id": f"req-semantic-{self.__class__.calls}"
                    },
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=(
                        f"req-semantic-{self.__class__.calls}"
                    ),
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once(
            "worker-semantic",
            global_limit=1,
            model_client_type=SequentialClient,
        ) is True
        with sessions() as db:
            after_first = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert after_first is not None
            assert after_first.status == "retryable", (
                after_first.error_code,
                after_first.error_message,
            )
        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once(
            "worker-semantic",
            global_limit=1,
            model_client_type=SequentialClient,
        ) is True

        with sessions() as db:
            persisted_run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation)
                .where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)
            ).all()
            assert persisted_run is not None
            assert persisted_run.status == expected_run_status, (
                persisted_run.error_code,
                persisted_run.error_message,
            )
            assert SequentialClient.calls == 2
            assert [row.attempt_no for row in attempts] == [1, 2]
            assert attempts[0].status == "failed"
            assert json.loads(attempts[0].checkpoint_json)["status"] == (
                "failed_post_settlement"
            )
            assert len(invocations) == 2
            assert all(row.status == "succeeded" for row in invocations)
            assert all(row.input_tokens == 80 for row in invocations)
            assert all(row.output_tokens == 20 for row in invocations)
            assert all(row.actual_cost_micros > 0 for row in invocations)
            first_evidence = json.loads(invocations[0].evidence_json)["result"]
            assert first_evidence["semantic_validation"] == {
                "status": "rejected",
                "reasons": [
                    "redline_reason_conflict:large_area_watermark",
                    "evidence_conflict:redline:large_area_watermark",
                ],
            }
            if second_inconsistent:
                assert attempts[1].status == "failed"
                assert attempts[1].last_error_code == (
                    "model_output_semantic_inconsistency"
                )
                assert json.loads(invocations[1].evidence_json)["result"][
                    "semantic_validation"
                ]["status"] == "rejected"
            else:
                assert attempts[1].status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_semantic_validator_error_still_settles_provider_usage(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'semantic-validator-error.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            contract = _latest_inspiration_single_contract()
            node["config"]["output_contract"] = contract
            node["output_schema"] = deepcopy(contract["schema"])
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            manifest = run_snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]
            manifest["nodes"] = [node]
            manifest["resource_policy"] = {
                "source_provenance": {
                    "contract_profile": LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
                }
            }
            encoded_snapshot = canonical_json(run_snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded_snapshot
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                encoded_snapshot.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        def broken_validator(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("validator bug must not erase provider usage")

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "inspiration_semantic_conflicts",
            broken_validator,
        )

        class ConsistentClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=_latest_inspiration_single_payload(
                        inconsistent=False
                    ),
                    raw_text="{}",
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-semantic-validator-error"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-semantic-validator-error",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=90,
                    output_tokens=30,
                    total_tokens=120,
                )

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as captured:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=ConsistentClient,
            )

        assert captured.value.code == "model_output_semantic_validation_failed"
        assert captured.value.retryable is False
        assert captured.value.failure_phase == "post_settlement"
        with sessions() as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "succeeded"
            assert invocation.input_tokens == 90
            assert invocation.output_tokens == 30
            assert invocation.actual_cost_micros > 0
            assert json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)["result"][
                "semantic_validation"
            ] == {
                "status": "error",
                "error_type": "RuntimeError",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_foreach_executes_each_frozen_image_once_and_keeps_parent_open(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-foreach.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    provider_calls: list[str] = []
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, prompt_node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch, strict_output=True
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            item_executor = dict(prompt_node["config"])
            item_executor["kind"] = "prompt"
            foreach_node = {
                **prompt_node,
                "kind": "foreach_image",
                "config": {
                    "items_path": "$.case.images",
                    "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                    "max_items": 2,
                    "max_parallel_items": 1,
                    "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                    "item_executor": item_executor,
                },
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            }
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [foreach_node]
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_budget"].update(
                {
                    "max_requests": 2,
                    "max_input_tokens": 24_000,
                    "max_output_tokens": 1_024,
                }
            )
            child = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
            parent_input = {
                "case": {
                    "images": [
                        {**child, "cloud_id": "image-a"},
                        {**child, "cloud_id": "image-b"},
                    ]
                }
            }
            attempt.input_manifest_json = canonical_json(parent_input)
            attempt.input_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                attempt.input_manifest_json.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                run.snapshot_json.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            run_id = run.id
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                call = f"call-{len(provider_calls) + 1}"
                provider_calls.append(call)
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": call},
                    raw_text=canonical_json({"score": 88, "label": call}),
                    raw_payload={"id": call},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=call,
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        output = fixture._real_foreach_result(
            worker_id="worker-1",
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            run_id=run_id,
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
            node=foreach_node,
            input_manifest=parent_input,
            model_client_type=SuccessClient,
            input_fingerprint="f" * 64,
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )

        assert provider_calls == ["call-1", "call-2"], canonical_json(
            output.output_manifest
        )
        assert output.output_manifest["schema_version"] == "workflow-node-result-v1"
        assert output.output_manifest["node_instance_id"] == foreach_node[
            "node_instance_id"
        ]
        assert output.output_manifest["output"]["counts"]["succeeded"] == 2
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).status == "running"
            children = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowForeachItem).order_by(
                    WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal
                )
            ).all()
            assert [row.status for row in children] == [
                "succeeded",
                "succeeded",
            ]
            assert [
                row.invocation_key
                for row in db.scalars(
                    select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(
                        WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key
                    )
                )
            ] == ["item:image-a:attempt:1", "item:image-b:attempt:1"]
            media = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage)).all()
            assert {
                json.loads(row.evidence_json)["workflow_invocation"][
                    "foreach_item_key"
                ]
                for row in media
            } == {"image-a", "image-b"}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_foreach_fail_open_closes_provider_call_when_settlement_fails(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-foreach-settlement.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, prompt_node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch, strict_output=True
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            item_executor = dict(prompt_node["config"])
            item_executor["kind"] = "prompt"
            foreach_node = {
                **prompt_node,
                "kind": "foreach_image",
                "config": {
                    "items_path": "$.case.images",
                    "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                    "max_items": 1,
                    "max_parallel_items": 1,
                    "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                    "item_executor": item_executor,
                },
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            }
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [foreach_node]
            child = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
            parent_input = {
                "case": {"images": [{**child, "cloud_id": "image-a"}]}
            }
            attempt.input_manifest_json = canonical_json(parent_input)
            attempt.input_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                attempt.input_manifest_json.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                run.snapshot_json.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            run_id = run.id
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "provider-returned"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"provider-returned"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-provider-returned"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-provider-returned",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        def fail_settlement(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_pricing_settlement_invalid",
                "结算快照损坏",
                status_code=409,
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "settle_model_invocation",
            fail_settlement,
        )

        output = fixture._real_foreach_result(
            worker_id="worker-1",
            attempt_id=attempt_id,
            lease_token=lease_token,
            run_id=run_id,
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
            node=foreach_node,
            input_manifest=parent_input,
            model_client_type=SuccessClient,
            input_fingerprint="f" * 64,
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )

        assert output.output_manifest["output"]["counts"] == {
            "total": 1,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 1,
            "skipped": 0,
        }
        with sessions() as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_schema_mismatch_failure_evidence_carries_raw_excerpt() -> None:
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import _failure_raw_excerpt
    from app.workflow_model_executor import WorkflowModelExecutorError

    error = WorkflowModelExecutorError(
        "structured_output_schema_mismatch", "x", retryable=True,
        evidence={"raw_excerpt": '{"tags": "a,b,c"}'},
    )
    assert _failure_raw_excerpt(error) == {"raw_excerpt": '{"tags": "a,b,c"}'}
    assert _failure_raw_excerpt(ValueError("plain")) == {}
    assert _failure_raw_excerpt(WorkflowModelExecutorError("c", "m")) == {}


def test_real_prompt_freezes_bound_doubao_endpoint_on_run(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    """准备阶段解析到的接入点必须落库到运行上，改绑后同一运行仍沿用原接入点。"""

    import app.doubao_endpoints as endpoints_module
    from app.doubao_endpoint_models import DoubaoInferenceEndpoint
    from app.models import WorkflowDefinition

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-endpoint.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
            endpoint = DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(
                endpoint_id="ep-20260907171239-krrnx", display_name="探针", category_key=run.category_key,
                created_by="t", updated_by="t",
            )
            db.add(endpoint)
            db.flush()
            db.get(WorkflowDefinition, run.workflow_definition_id).doubao_endpoint_row_id = endpoint.id
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id, lease_token, run_id = attempt.id, attempt.lease_token, run.id
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        # 夹具模型不是豆包 2.0 身份；这里只验证冻结落库，把 2.0 判定打桩为真
        monkeypatch.setattr(endpoints_module, "is_doubao_seed_2_model", lambda *_a, **_k: True)
        seen: list[str | None] = []

        class RecordingTimeoutClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                seen.append(getattr(config, "endpoint_id", None))

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_options):
                raise DoubaoTransportError("timeout")

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError):
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1", attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=lease_token, run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node, input_manifest=input_manifest, model_client_type=RecordingTimeoutClient,
            )
        assert seen == ["ep-20260907171239-krrnx"]
        with Session(engine) as db:
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).doubao_endpoint_id == "ep-20260907171239-krrnx"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_pool_timeout_after_dispatch_hook_rolls_back_to_not_dispatched(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    """连接池排队超时：dispatch hook 已触发但请求未发出，记 rejected、不记 wait_cap、不留费用责任。"""

    from app.doubao import DoubaoPoolTimeoutError

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-pool-timeout.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt_id, lease_token = attempt.id, attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class PoolTimeoutClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                self.hook = None

            def set_dispatch_hook(self, hook):
                self.hook = hook

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_options):
                self.hook()  # 与真实客户端一致：hook 在 client.post 之前触发
                raise DoubaoPoolTimeoutError()

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as caught:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1", attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=lease_token, run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node, input_manifest=input_manifest, model_client_type=PoolTimeoutClient,
            )
        assert caught.value.code == "model_provider_pool_timeout"
        assert caught.value.retryable is True
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation.status == "rejected"
            failure = json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)["failure"]
            assert "wait_cap_hit" not in failure
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("timeout_origin", ["transport", "runtime_deadline"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("attempt_number,expected_retry", [(1, True), (2, False)])
def test_opt_in_batch_timeout_retains_unknown_cost_and_bounds_recovery(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path, attempt_number, expected_retry, timeout_origin,
) -> None:
    """Opt-in timeout queues one delayed attempt without releasing old cost."""

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_TIMEOUT_RETRY", "true")
    original_prepare = fixture.prepare_prompt_invocation
    def prepare_batch(*args, **kwargs):
        prepared = original_prepare(*args, **kwargs)
        prepared.client_config.api_path = "/batch/chat/completions"
        if timeout_origin == "runtime_deadline": prepared.client_config.timeout_seconds=.01
        return prepared
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "prepare_prompt_invocation", prepare_batch)
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-timeout.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            node['retry_policy']={'kind':'fixed','max_attempts':2,'delay_seconds':5}
            attempt.script_version.max_attempts=2
            run_snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            run_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes']=[node]
            run.snapshot_json=canonical_json(run_snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt.attempt_no = attempt_number
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            db.commit()
        assert lease_token is not None
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)

        class TimeoutClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                pass

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_options):
                if timeout_origin == "runtime_deadline":
                    import asyncio
                    await asyncio.sleep(10)
                raise DoubaoTransportError("timeout")

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as exc_info:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(
                worker_id="worker-1",
                attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token,
                run_snapshot=run_snapshot,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                model_client_type=TimeoutClient,
            )

        assert exc_info.value.code == ("model_batch_timeout_recovery" if expected_retry else "model_invocation_outcome_unknown")
        assert exc_info.value.retry_after_seconds == (300 if expected_retry else None)
        from app.runtime_queue import RuntimeFailureClassifier
        assert RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(exc_info.value, error_code=exc_info.value.code).retryable is expected_retry
        assert exc_info.value.retryable is expected_retry
        fixture._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(
            attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=lease_token, exc=exc_info.value)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            latest=db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id.desc()))
            assert latest.status == ('retryable' if expected_retry else 'failed')
            if expected_retry:
                dispatch=db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id==latest.id))
                previous=db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,attempt_id)
                assert (dispatch.available_at-previous.finished_at).total_seconds()==300
                assert latest.attempt_no==2
            assert len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all())==1
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            assert json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)["failure"] == {
                "attempt_count": 0,
                "retryable": expected_retry,
                "technical_error_type": "timeout",
                        "detail": "模型请求等待截止" if timeout_origin == "runtime_deadline" else "模型 API timeout",
        }
            ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
            assert ledger is not None
            assert ledger.request_count == 1
            assert ledger.reserved_input_tokens == 12_000
            assert ledger.reserved_output_tokens == 512
            assert ledger.estimated_cost_micros == 13_024
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _transient_heartbeat_error(code: int = 1205) -> OperationalError:
    from pymysql.err import OperationalError as MysqlError

    return OperationalError(
        "UPDATE runtime_dispatch_items", {}, MysqlError(code, "transient")
    )


class _HeartbeatSession:
    def commit(self):
        pass

    def rollback(self):
        pass

    def close(self):
        pass


def test_runtime_heartbeat_tolerates_transient_database_errors(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """A lock wait/deadlock/disconnect must not abort a paid provider call."""
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    clock = {"now": 1000.0}
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture.time, "monotonic", lambda: clock["now"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", _HeartbeatSession)
    outcomes = [
        _transient_heartbeat_error(1205),
        _transient_heartbeat_error(1213),
        None,
        _transient_heartbeat_error(2013),
        _transient_heartbeat_error(2006),
    ]

    def heartbeat(*_args, **_kwargs):
        outcome = outcomes.pop(0)
        if outcome is not None:
            raise outcome

    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "heartbeat_runtime_step", heartbeat)
    beat = fixture._RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(
        attempt_id=1, lease_token="t", worker_id="w", lease_seconds=300,
    )
    for _ in range(5):
        clock["now"] += 60
        beat.tick()  # every failure is within half the lease since last success
    assert outcomes == []


def test_runtime_heartbeat_stops_when_lease_may_expire_or_is_lost(monkeypatch) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    clock = {"now": 1000.0}
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture.time, "monotonic", lambda: clock["now"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", _HeartbeatSession)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        fixture, "heartbeat_runtime_step",
        lambda *_a, **_k: (_ for _ in ()).throw(_transient_heartbeat_error()),
    )
    beat = fixture._RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(
        attempt_id=1, lease_token="t", worker_id="w", lease_seconds=300,
    )
    clock["now"] += 60
    beat.tick()
    clock["now"] += 60
    beat.tick()
    clock["now"] += 60
    with pytest.raises(OperationalError):
        beat.tick()

    lost = fixture.WorkflowRuntimeError("step_lease_stale", "lost", status_code=409)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        fixture, "heartbeat_runtime_step",
        lambda *_a, **_k: (_ for _ in ()).throw(lost),
    )
    fresh = fixture._RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(
        attempt_id=1, lease_token="t", worker_id="w", lease_seconds=300,
    )
    with pytest.raises(fixture.WorkflowRuntimeError):
        fresh.tick()


def test_runtime_heartbeat_thread_keeps_running_after_transient_error(monkeypatch) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "_PROVIDER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS", 0.01)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", _HeartbeatSession)
    calls = {"n": 0}
    import threading
    done = threading.Event()

    def heartbeat(*_args, **_kwargs):
        calls["n"] += 1
        if calls["n"] == 1:
            raise _transient_heartbeat_error(1213)
        if calls["n"] >= 3:
            done.set()

    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "heartbeat_runtime_step", heartbeat)
    beat = fixture._RuntimeLeaseHeartbeat(
        attempt_id=1, lease_token="t", worker_id="w", lease_seconds=300,
    )
    beat.start()
    assert done.wait(5)
    beat.stop()  # must not re-raise the tolerated transient error

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_foreach_executor.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.database import Base
from app.models import WorkflowForeachItem, WorkflowModelInvocation


def _executor_module():
    from app import workflow_foreach_executor

    return workflow_foreach_executor


@pytest.fixture
def session_factory(tmp_path: Path) -> Iterator[sessionmaker[Session]]:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield factory
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _node(*, max_items: int = 100, failure_policy: str = "fail_open"):
    return {
        "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
        "kind": "foreach_image",
        "retry_policy": {"max_attempts": 2},
        "config": {
            "items_path": "$.case.images",
            "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
            "max_items": max_items,
            "max_parallel_items": 4,
            "failure_policy": failure_policy,
            "item_executor": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "prompt_version_id": 17,
                "model_registry_id": 9,
                "thinking_enabled": False,
                "thinking_level": "none",
                "output_contract": {
                    "mode": "json_schema",
                    "schema": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}},
                        "required": ["score"],
                        "additionalProperties": False,
                    },
                },
            },
        },
    }


def _input(*keys: str):
    return {
        "case": {
            "case_id": "case-1",
            "images": [
                {
                    "cloud_id": key,
                    "asset_id": index + 1,
                    "asset_sha256": f"{index + 1:064x}",
                }
                for index, key in enumerate(keys)
            ],
        }
    }


def test_materialization_is_idempotent_and_duplicate_item_keys_fail(
    session_factory,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    with session_factory() as db:
        first = foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
            db,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        )
        db.commit()
        second = foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
            db,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        )
        db.commit()

        assert [item.item_key for item in first] == ["a", "b"]
        assert [item.id for item in second] == [item.id for item in first]
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowForeachItem.id))) == 2

        with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as raised:
            foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
                db,
                production_run_id=2,
                parent_step_attempt_id=8,
                node=_node(),
                input_manifest=_input("same", "same"),
            )
        assert raised.value.code == "foreach_item_key_duplicate"
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count(WorkflowForeachItem.id)).where(
                WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id == 8
            )
        ) == 0


def test_resume_never_reinvokes_succeeded_child(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    invocation_keys: list[str] = []

    def invoke(child):
        invocation_keys.append(child.invocation_key)
        return {"score": 0.9 if child.item_key == "a" else 0.8}

    first = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker-1",
        invoke_item=invoke,
        max_claims=1,
    )
    assert first is None

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker-2",
        invoke_item=invoke,
    )

    assert invocation_keys == ["item:a:attempt:1", "item:b:attempt:1"]
    assert output is not None
    assert output["counts"] == {
        "total": 2,
        "succeeded": 2,
        "failed": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
    }


def test_parent_heartbeat_runs_before_every_foreach_child(
    session_factory,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    heartbeats: list[str] = []

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,

        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker",
        invoke_item=lambda child: {"score": child.ordinal},
        heartbeat_parent=lambda: heartbeats.append("heartbeat"),
    )

    assert output is not None
    assert len(heartbeats) == 2


def test_fail_open_preserves_failure_and_completes_parent(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()

    def invoke(child):
        if child.item_key == "b":
            raise foreach.ForeachItemExecutionError(
                "provider_rejected",
                "供应商拒绝该图片",
                retryable=False,
            )
        return {"score": 0.75}

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(failure_policy="fail_open"),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker",
        invoke_item=invoke,
    )

    assert output is not None
    assert output["schema_version"] == "workflow-foreach-output-v1"
    assert output["counts"] == {
        "total": 2,
        "succeeded": 1,
        "failed": 1,
        "skipped": 0,
    }
    assert [(item["item_key"], item["status"]) for item in output["items"]] == [
        ("a", "succeeded"),
        ("b", "failed"),
    ]
    assert output["items"][1]["error"]["code"] == "provider_rejected"


def test_fail_closed_terminates_after_bounded_retries(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    invocation_keys: list[str] = []

    def invoke(child):
        invocation_keys.append(child.invocation_key)
        raise foreach.ForeachItemExecutionError(
            "provider_429",
            "供应商限流",
            retryable=True,
        )

    with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as raised:
        foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
            session_factory,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(failure_policy="fail_closed"),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
            worker_id="worker",
            invoke_item=invoke,
        )

    assert raised.value.code == "foreach_child_failed"
    assert invocation_keys == ["item:a:attempt:1", "item:a:attempt:2"]
    assert raised.value.output_manifest["counts"] == {
        "total": 2,
        "succeeded": 0,
        "failed": 1,
        "skipped": 1,
    }

    with session_factory() as db:
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowForeachItem).order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal)
        ).all()
        assert [row.status for row in rows] == ["failed", "skipped"]
        assert rows[0].attempt_count == 2


def test_quota_wait_yields_parent_without_spending_child_attempt(session_factory):
    foreach = _executor_module()
    keys = []
    def invoke(child):
        keys.append(child.invocation_key)
        raise foreach.ForeachItemExecutionError("quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted",
            "local capacity", retryable=True)
    kwargs = dict(production_run_id=1, parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(failure_policy="fail_closed"), input_manifest=_input("a"),
        worker_id="worker", invoke_item=invoke)
    for _ in range(4):
        with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as error:
            foreach.execute_foreach_parent(session_factory, **kwargs)
        assert error.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
    assert keys == ["item:a:attempt:1"] * 4
    with session_factory() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowForeachItem))
        assert row.status == "retryable"
        assert row.attempt_count == 0

    foreach = _executor_module()
