def test_real_text_prompt_uses_frozen_context_without_requiring_an_image(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'text-model-executor.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot_node = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]
            snapshot_node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
            frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
            frozen_input.pop("asset_id")
            frozen_input.pop("asset_sha256")
            frozen_input["case_metadata"] = {
                "case_id": "case-42",
                "image_results": [{"score": 91}, {"score": 87}],
            }
            encoded = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(encoded)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert prepared.image_path is None
            assert prepared.mime_type is None
            assert prepared.image_samples is None
            assert prepared.media_bundle.items == ()
            assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == ()
            assert '"case_id":"case-42"' in prepared.user_prompt
            assert '"score":42' in prepared.user_prompt

            calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

            class FakeTextClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    calls.append({
                        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                        "user_prompt": user_prompt,
                        "options": options,
                    })
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 89.0, "label": "通过"},
                        raw_text='{"score":89.0,"label":"通过"}',
                        raw_payload={},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-text-1",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=90,
                        output_tokens=20,
                        total_tokens=110,
                    )

            result = asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=FakeTextClient)
            )

            assert result.node_result["output"]["label"] == "通过"
            assert calls[0]["options"]["image_path"] is None
            assert calls[0]["options"]["mime_type"] is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_executor_passes_and_enforces_frozen_output_contract(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-output-contract.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db,
                tmp_path,
                monkeypatch,
                strict_output=True,
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            captured: dict[str, Any] = {}

            class InvalidSchemaClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                    captured.update(options)
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好", "extra": True},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好","extra":true}',
                        raw_payload={},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-schema-mismatch",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=10,
                        output_tokens=5,
                        total_tokens=15,
                    )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                asyncio.run(
                    execute_prepared_prompt(
                        prepared,
                        client_type=InvalidSchemaClient,
                    )
                )

            assert captured["output_contract"].mode == "json_schema"
            assert captured["output_contract"].strict is True
            assert exc_info.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"
            assert exc_info.value.retryable is True
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_paid_acceptance_campaign_wraps_provider_dispatch_and_settlement(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches provider I/O happening outside the campaign-wide call ledger."""

    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor
    from app.models import PaidAcceptanceCallReservation
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-campaign-success.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CAMPAIGN_ID", "macbook-20260829")
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CALL_LIMIT", "2")
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest, raising=False
        )

        class FakeClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                pass

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                    raw_payload={},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-campaign-safe",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=512,
                    reasoning_effort="high",
                    thinking_mode="enabled",
                    input_tokens=10,
                    output_tokens=5,
                    total_tokens=15,
                )

        asyncio.run(
            execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=FakeClient)
        )

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            reservation = db.scalar(select(PaidAcceptanceCallReservation))
            assert reservation is not None
            assert reservation.attempt_key == f"attempt:{attempt.id}"
            assert reservation.ordinal == 1
            assert reservation.status == "succeeded"
            assert reservation.provider_correlation_id == "req-campaign-safe"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_paid_acceptance_exhaustion_blocks_client_construction(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches checking the hard ceiling only after creating a network client."""

    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor
    from app.paid_acceptance_guard import reserve_acceptance_call
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-campaign-full.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    calls: list[str] = []
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            reserve_acceptance_call(
                db,
                campaign_id="macbook-20260829",
                attempt_key="attempt:already-charged",
                limit=1,
            )
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CAMPAIGN_ID", "macbook-20260829")
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CALL_LIMIT", "1")
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest, raising=False
        )

        class MustNotConstructClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                calls.append("constructed")

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
                raise AssertionError("provider call must be blocked")

        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
            asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(
                    prepared,
                    client_type=MustNotConstructClient,
                )
            )
        assert exc_info.value.code == "acceptance_call_limit_exhausted"
        assert calls == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("input_modality,context_mode", [
    ("image", None), ("text", None), ("image", "full_v1"), ("image", "routing_only_v1"),
])
def test_authoritative_prompt_b_uses_frozen_rules_precheck_and_anchor_images(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    input_modality: str,
    context_mode: str | None,
) -> None:
    """Native A/B runs must preserve the legacy B prompt and anchor contract."""

    from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
        build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    )
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'model-authoritative-b-{input_modality}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)

            node["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_b"
            component = node["config"]["component_snapshot"]
            component["version"] = "prompt-b-frozen-v1"
            component["content"] = {
                "stage": "B",
                "system_prompt": "冻结 B 系统正文",
                "user_prompt": (
                    "调用 A：{{precheck_json}}\n"
                    "上一步：{{previous_output}}\n"
                    "规则：{{dimension_rules}}\n"
                    "输出：{{response_contract}}"
                ),
                "rubric_version": "rubric-v7",
            }
            component["content_hash"] = canonical_component_hash(
                component["content"]
            )

            prompt_a_node = deepcopy(node)
            prompt_a_node["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content"][
                "stage"
            ] = "A"
            if context_mode is not None:
                node["config"]["precheck_context_mode"] = context_mode

            dimensions = build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()
            dimension_content = {"subcategory_dimensions": dimensions}
            dimension_node = {
                "node_instance_id": "dimension_scoring_5",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 501,
                        "version": "17",
                        "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring",
                        "content_json": canonical_json(dimension_content),
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                            dimension_content
                        ),
                    },
                },
            }

            anchor_assets = []
            anchors = []
            for index, level in enumerate(("L1", "L2"), start=1):
                path = tmp_path / f"anchor-{index}.jpg"
                Image.new(
                    "RGB",
                    (640 + index, 480 + index),
                    (index, index + 1, index + 2),
                ).save(path, format="JPEG")
                raw = path.read_bytes()
                asset = Asset(
                    original_name=path.name,
                    stored_name=path.name,
                    mime_type="image/jpeg",
                    size_bytes=len(raw),
                    sha256=_sha256(raw),
                    category_key="space_image",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                anchor_assets.append(asset)
                anchors.append(
                    {
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "level": level,
                        "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
                        "sha256": asset.sha256,
                    }
                )
            anchor_content = {
                "spec_version": "anchor-mechanism-v1",
                "enabled": True,
                "max_anchor_images": 2,
                "anchors": anchors,
            }
            anchor_node = {
                "node_instance_id": "anchor_calibration_6",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 601,
                        "version": "4",
                        "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                        "content_json": canonical_json(anchor_content),
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(anchor_content),
                    },
                },
            }

            prior_output = {
                "track_classification": "class_one",
                "classification_confidence": 0.96,
                "primary_category": "景观设计",
                "redline_triggered": {},
                "reason": ["A独有的主观评语标记"],
                "hard_defects": [],
                "image_defects": [],
                "decisive_evidence": {
                    "redline_triggered": {},
                    "hard_defects": [],
                    "image_defects": [],
                },
                "decision_status": "complete",
                "uncertain_fields": [],
            }
            prior_result = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                "node_kind": "prompt",
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": prior_output,
            }
            prior_attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id,
                step_key="prompt_a",
                step_type="model_call",
                sequence=0,
                script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
                status="succeeded",
                attempt_no=1,
                idempotency_key=f"{run.run_key}:prompt_a:attempt:1",
                input_manifest_json="{}",
                input_hash=_sha256("{}"),
                output_manifest_json=canonical_json(prior_result),
                output_hash=_sha256(canonical_json(prior_result)),
                checkpoint_json="{}",
            )
            db.add(prior_attempt)

            direct_input = {
                "upstream": {
                    "track_routing_2": {
                        "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                        "output": {"track_key": "", "confidence": "low"},
                    }
                }
            }
            attempt.input_manifest_json = canonical_json(direct_input)
            attempt.input_hash = _sha256(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt.sequence = 4

            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["category_key"] = (
                "inspiration_image"
            )
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [
                prompt_a_node,
                node,
                dimension_node,
                anchor_node,
            ]
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["category_key"] = "inspiration_image"
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"] = {
                "prompt_frozen": snapshot["runtime_context"][
                    "real_execution_node_limits"
                ]["prompt_frozen"]
            }
            run.category_key = "inspiration_image"
            if input_modality == "text":
                node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                for manifest_node in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]:
                    if manifest_node.get("node_instance_id") == node["node_instance_id"]:
                        manifest_node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
                frozen_input.pop("asset_id")
                frozen_input.pop("asset_sha256")
                frozen_input["case_metadata"] = {"case_id": "text-case-b"}
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=direct_input,
            )

            assert prepared.system_prompt == "冻结 B 系统正文"
            if context_mode == "routing_only_v1":
                assert "routing_only_v1" in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "class_one" in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "A独有的主观评语标记" not in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "景观设计" not in prepared.user_prompt
            else:
                assert "景观设计" in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "A独有的主观评语标记" in prepared.user_prompt
            # Transport projection must not rewrite the authoritative A checkpoint.
            assert prior_attempt.output_manifest_json == canonical_json(prior_result)
            assert prior_attempt.output_hash == _sha256(canonical_json(prior_result))
            assert '"track_key":""' not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "visual_structure" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "hit_rules" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "{{" not in prepared.user_prompt
            if input_modality == "image":
                assert prepared.image_samples is not None
                assert len(prepared.media_bundle.anchors) == 2
                assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == (
                    "anchor:0",
                    "anchor:1",
                    "target:0",
                )
                assert [sample[0] for sample in prepared.image_samples] == [
                    f"锚点图 L1（素材 {anchor_assets[0].id}）",
                    f"锚点图 L2（素材 {anchor_assets[1].id}）",
                    "待评图片（禁止把锚点图等级直接当作输出）",
                ]
            else:
                assert prepared.image_samples is None
                assert prepared.media_bundle.items == ()
                assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == ()

            captured: dict[str, Any] = {}
            cache_usage = CacheUsageObservation(
                status="hit",
                cached_input_tokens=256,
                source="usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
            )

            class FakeAnchorClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json_images(
                    self, system_prompt, samples, **options
                ):
                    captured.update(
                        {
                            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                            "samples": samples,
                            "options": options,
                        }
                    )
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={"usage": {}},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-anchor-safe",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_image_tokens=101,
                        per_image_tokens=(20, 30, 51),
                        input_tokens=321,
                        output_tokens=45,
                        total_tokens=366,
                        cache_usage=cache_usage,
                    )

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    captured.update(
                        {
                            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                            "samples": None,
                            "options": {**options, "user_prompt": user_prompt},
                        }
                    )
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={"usage": {}},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-text-safe",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=321,
                        output_tokens=45,
                        total_tokens=366,
                        cache_usage=cache_usage,
                    )

            result = asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(
                    prepared,
                    client_type=FakeAnchorClient,
                )
            )

            assert captured["samples"] == (
                list(prepared.image_samples)
                if prepared.image_samples is not None
                else None
            )
            assert captured["options"]["user_prompt"] == prepared.user_prompt
            assert result.cache_usage == cache_usage
            assert result.invocation_evidence["cache_observation"] == {
                "status": "hit",
                "cached_input_tokens": 256,
                "source": "usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
            }
            if input_modality == "image":
                assert captured["options"]["max_image_count"] == 3
                assert result.media_usage["tokens_by_identity"] == {
                    "anchor:0": 20,
                    "anchor:1": 30,
                    "target:0": 51,
                }
                assert result.media_usage["total_image_tokens"] == 101
            else:
                assert "tokens_by_identity" not in result.media_usage
                assert "total_image_tokens" not in result.media_usage
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("input_modality", ["image", "text"])
def test_inspiration_prompt_a_receives_the_frozen_redline_fact_contract(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    input_modality: str,
) -> None:
    """Call A must answer the rule module's current keys, not an old hardcode."""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'model-authoritative-a-{input_modality}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            redline_content = {
                "format_version": "redline-policy-v1",
                "enabled": True,
                "hit_level": "L5",
                "hit_score_cap": 49,
                "rules": [
                    {
                        "key": "transparent_checkerboard",
                        "signal": "production_fields.reason",
                        "match_any": ["透明棋盘格"],
                        "exemptions": [],
                        "enabled": True,
                    }
                ],
            }
            redline_node = {
                "node_instance_id": "eligibility_redline_1",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 701,
                        "version": "5",
                        "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                        "content_json": canonical_json(redline_content),
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(redline_content),
                    },
                },
            }
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["category_key"] = (
                "inspiration_image"
            )
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"].append(redline_node)
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["category_key"] = "inspiration_image"
            run.category_key = "inspiration_image"
            if input_modality == "text":
                node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                snapshot_node = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]
                snapshot_node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
                frozen_input.pop("asset_id")
                frozen_input.pop("asset_sha256")
                frozen_input["case_metadata"] = {"case_id": "text-case-a"}
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert "灵感图权威前检合同" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "transparent_checkerboard" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "透明棋盘格" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "只返回可审计事实，不评分" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert (prepared.image_path is None) is (input_modality == "text")
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_authoritative_prompt_b_rejects_tampered_prompt_a_checkpoint(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Call B must not consume a succeeded Call-A row whose hash no longer matches."""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        _prior_prompt_a_output,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'tampered-prompt-a.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prompt_a_node = deepcopy(node)
            prompt_a_node["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content"][
                "stage"
            ] = "A"
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "asset_key"
            ] = "prompt_b"
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "component_snapshot"
            ]["content"]["stage"] = "B"
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"].append(prompt_a_node)
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            result = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                "node_kind": "prompt",
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": {"track_classification": "class_one"},
            }
            db.add(
                ProductionStepAttempt(
                    run_id=run.id,
                    step_key="prompt_a",
                    step_type="model_call",
                    sequence=0,
                    script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
                    status="succeeded",
                    attempt_no=1,
                    idempotency_key=f"{run.run_key}:prompt_a:tampered",
                    input_manifest_json="{}",
                    input_hash=_sha256("{}"),
                    output_manifest_json=canonical_json(result),
                    output_hash="0" * 64,
                    checkpoint_json="{}",
                )
            )
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                _prior_prompt_a_output(db, run=run, snapshot=snapshot)

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_authoritative_prompt_a_invalid"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_prepare_prompt_invocation_rejects_model_transport_drift(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """冻结 workflow 不能把当前凭据发送到不同的供应商端点。"""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            model.base_url = "https://changed-endpoint.invalid"
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                prepare_prompt_invocation(
                    db,
                    run=run,
                    attempt=attempt,
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_config_snapshot_drift"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_prepare_prompt_invocation_uses_frozen_output_params_after_config_changes(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """评测机制版本应继续使用冻结参数，不受当前全局输出参数变化影响。"""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'frozen-output-params.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            model.max_tokens = 8_192
            model.temperature = 0.9
            model.thinking_mode = "disabled"
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert prepared.output_budget == 512
            assert prepared.client_config.max_tokens == 512
            assert prepared.client_config.temperature == 0.25
            assert prepared.client_config.thinking_mode == "enabled"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_real_prompt_executor_rejects_hidden_provider_retries(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches a client reporting more than the one authorized paid attempt."""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-hidden-retry.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            class RetriedClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_options):
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 80, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":80,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={"usage": {}},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-retried",
                        attempt_count=2,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=100,
                        output_tokens=20,
                        total_tokens=120,
                    )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                asyncio.run(
                    execute_prepared_prompt(
                        prepared,
                        client_type=RetriedClient,
                    )
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_response_controls_mismatch"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("primary_category", [None, "家装"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("angle_version", [False, True, "custom"])
def test_knowledge_graph_real_prompt_maps_asset_id_and_preserves_route_contract(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    primary_category,
    angle_version,
) -> None:
    """Catches run385's unresolved PRIMARY_CATEGORY without leaking golden truth."""

    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-kg.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db,
                tmp_path,
                monkeypatch,
                knowledge_graph=True,
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            contract_key = "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"
            if angle_version:
                from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract, knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract
                contract_key = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v2"
                contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key)
                if angle_version == "custom":
                    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import extend_knowledge_graph_contract
                    schema = contract.output_schema
                    schema["properties"]["review_required"] = {"type": "boolean"}
                    schema["properties"]["single_result"] = {"type": "object", "properties": {"confidence": {"type": "number"}}, "required": ["confidence"], "additionalProperties": False}
                    schema["required"] += ["review_required", "single_result"]
                    contract = extend_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key, schema)
                node["config"].update({
                    "output_contract_key": contract_key,
                    "output_contract_snapshot": contract.snapshot(),
                    "output_contract": knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(contract_key, contract.snapshot()),
                })
                node["output_schema"] = contract.output_schema
                snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [node]
            frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
            frozen_input["expected_result"] = {"primary_category": "不应读入的人工真值"}
            if primary_category is not None:
                frozen_input["primary_category"] = primary_category
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
                "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
                contract_key,
                expected_asset_id="ll-frozen-1",
                snapshot=node["config"].get("output_contract_snapshot"),
            )
            if angle_version:
                expected["view_angle"] = "正视"
            expected["material"] = "wood,brushed metal"
            raw_model = {**expected, "material": "wood, brushed metal"}
            captured: dict[str, Any] = {}

            class FakeDoubaoClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    captured.update(
                        {
                            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                            "user_prompt": user_prompt,
                            "options": options,
                        }
                    )
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed=raw_model,
                        raw_text=canonical_json(raw_model),
                        raw_payload={"choices": [], "usage": {}},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-kg-1",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=321,
                        output_tokens=210,
                        total_tokens=531,
                    )

            result = asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(
                    prepared,
                    client_type=FakeDoubaoClient,
                )
            )

            assert "{{ASSET_ID}}" not in captured["system_prompt"]
            assert "ll-frozen-1" in captured["system_prompt"]
            assert "{{PRIMARY_CATEGORY}}" not in captured["user_prompt"]
            assert (primary_category or "未提供（不满足家装或工装条件）") in captured["user_prompt"]
            assert result.node_result["output"] == expected
            assert result.node_result["model_invocation"]["parsed_output"]["material"] == "wood, brushed metal"
            assert "material:kg_comma_spacing_normalized" in result.node_result["model_invocation"]["coercions"]
            assert result.node_result["output_contract_key"] == contract_key
            if angle_version:
                assert result.node_result["model_invocation"]["parsed_output"]["view_angle"] == "正视"
                assert result.node_result["knowledge_graph_mapping"]["semantic"]["view_angle"][0]["value"] == "正视"
            mapping = result.node_result["knowledge_graph_mapping"]
            assert mapping["identity"] == {
                "field": "ll_id",
                "value": "ll-frozen-1",
                "is_single": 0,
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_oversized_asset_is_compressed_before_reaching_the_provider(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """超大图必须先压缩再发模型，与老评测路径行为一致。

    run136：两张 17.3MB / 13.5MB 的 PNG 被这条新路径原样发给豆包，直接 400
    拒收——老路径会先压到 8MB 以内。1.2% 的「模型拒绝」其实是平台没做预处理。
    """

    import io

    from PIL import Image

    from app.media import MODEL_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES
    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'oversized.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            # 把素材换成一张超过上限的真实 PNG（噪声图不可压，体积可控）。
            import os as _os

            noise = Image.frombytes(
                "RGB", (2200, 2200), _os.urandom(2200 * 2200 * 3)
            )
            big_path = tmp_path / "oversized.png"
            noise.save(big_path, format="PNG")
            assert big_path.stat().st_size > MODEL_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES
            raw = big_path.read_bytes()
            from sqlalchemy import select as _select

            asset = db.scalar(_select(Asset))
            assert asset is not None
            asset.stored_name = big_path.name
            asset.original_name = big_path.name
            asset.mime_type = "image/png"
            asset.size_bytes = len(raw)
            asset.sha256 = _sha256(raw)
            # 冻结输入在 run 快照的 runtime_context 里，改素材必须同步快照与哈希。
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["asset_sha256"] = (
                asset.sha256
            )
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert prepared.image_path != big_path, "超大图未经压缩直接发往模型"
            assert prepared.image_path.stat().st_size <= MODEL_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES
            assert prepared.mime_type == "image/jpeg"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()



def test_single_prompt_stage_attaches_anchors_like_authoritative_b() -> None:
    # 单提示词版合并了 A/B 职责、正文以 5 张锚点为基准，必须与权威 B 一样带锚点；
    # A 阶段与普通节点不带。
    from app.workflow_model_executor import _prompt_attaches_anchors

    assert _prompt_attaches_anchors({"stage": "B"}, {"asset_key": "prompt_b"}, authoritative=True)
    assert _prompt_attaches_anchors({"stage": "SINGLE"}, {"asset_key": "latest_single_prompt"}, authoritative=False)
    assert _prompt_attaches_anchors({"stage": "single"}, {}, authoritative=False)
    assert _prompt_attaches_anchors({}, {"asset_key": "latest_single_prompt"}, authoritative=False)
    assert not _prompt_attaches_anchors({"stage": "A"}, {"asset_key": "prompt_a"}, authoritative=False)
    assert not _prompt_attaches_anchors({}, {}, authoritative=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure_kind", ["kg", "structured", "missing_usage"])
def test_rejected_provider_output_preserves_billing_through_fixture_settlement(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, failure_kind
) -> None:
    """KG rejection/wrapped schema errors must not erase a charged response."""
    from app.doubao import DoubaoStructuredOutputError
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError, execute_prepared_prompt, prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
    from test_workflow_fixture_executor import _fixture_module, _convert_to_incremental_run

    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'rejected-usage.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch, knowledge_graph=True,
            )
            snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db, run=run, attempt=attempt, node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                db, attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node, run_snapshot=snapshot,
            )
            invocation_id = invocation.id
            db.commit()

        rejected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
            "kg.domestic.whole.v1", "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1",
            expected_asset_id="ll-frozen-1",
        )
        rejected["color"] = "米白色,黑色"

        class RejectedClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                if failure_kind == "structured":
                    error = DoubaoStructuredOutputError("provider schema mismatch")
                    error.provider_usage = {
                        "input_tokens": 321, "output_tokens": 210,
                        "cached_input_tokens": 128, "cache_observation_status": "hit",
                    }
                    error.request_correlation_id = "req-rejected-usage"
                    error.upstream_status_code = 200
                    error.attempt_count = 1
                    error.finish_reason = "stop"
                    error.raw_excerpt = '{"score":"invalid"}'
                    raise error
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=rejected, raw_text=canonical_json(rejected), raw_payload={},
                    upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id="req-rejected-usage",
                    attempt_count=1, output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=None if failure_kind == "missing_usage" else 321,
                    output_tokens=None if failure_kind == "missing_usage" else 210,
                    total_tokens=None if failure_kind == "missing_usage" else 531,
                    cache_usage=CacheUsageObservation(
                        status="hit", cached_input_tokens=128, source="usage",
                    ),
                )

        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as captured:
            asyncio.run(execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=RejectedClient))
        error = captured.value
        if failure_kind == "kg":
            assert "color" in str(error)
            assert error.evidence["rejected_model_output"]["color"] == "米白色,黑色"
        elif failure_kind == "structured":
            assert error.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"
            assert error.finish_reason == "stop"
        else:
            assert error.code == "model_usage_missing"
        assert error.request_correlation_id == "req-rejected-usage"
        assert error.upstream_status_code == 200
        assert error.attempt_count == 1
        # The real fixture path invokes fail_model_invocation. Replay must not bill twice.
        for _ in range(2):
            fixture._model_failure(
                invocation_id, code=error.code, message=str(error), retryable=error.retryable,
                error=error, provider_dispatched=True,
            )
        with sessions() as db:
            row = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
            assert row.status == "failed"
            assert row.request_correlation_id == "req-rejected-usage"
            assert row.upstream_status_code == 200
            failure = json.loads(row.evidence_json)["failure"]
            if failure_kind == "missing_usage":
                assert getattr(error, "provider_usage", None) is None
                assert row.input_tokens == 0
                assert row.actual_cost_micros == 0
                assert "settlement" not in json.loads(row.evidence_json)
                assert failure["usage_status"] == "pending_reconciliation"
            else:
                assert row.input_tokens == 321
                assert row.output_tokens == 210
                assert failure["provider_usage"]["cached_input_tokens"] == 128
                assert row.actual_cost_micros > 0
                assert failure["usage_status"] == "provider_reported"
                assert failure["provider_usage"]["cache_observation_status"] == "hit"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


# --- 多次采样投票 ---------------------------------------------------------------

def _vote_output(score, grades, deductions=(), confidence=0.9):
    dims = {k: {"grade": g, "evidence": f"e{k}{g}", "shortcomings": ""} for k, g in grades.items()}
    return {"aesthetic_score": score, "confidence": confidence, "dimensions": dims,
            "overall_evidence": f"score {score}", "source_deductions": [{"key": k, "evidence": k} for k in deductions],
            "contract_version": "inspiration-aesthetic-foundation-v3"}


def test_vote_aggregation_takes_medians_and_majority_deductions():
    from app.workflow_model_executor import aggregate_vote_outputs

    outputs = [
        _vote_output(88, {"a": 5, "b": 4}, deductions=("webpage_url",)),
        _vote_output(78, {"a": 4, "b": 4}, deductions=("webpage_url", "plain_background_single_object")),
        _vote_output(82, {"a": 4, "b": 3}),
    ]
    agg = aggregate_vote_outputs(outputs)
    assert agg["aesthetic_score"] == 82, "分数取中位数"
    assert agg["dimensions"]["a"]["grade"] == 4 and agg["dimensions"]["b"]["grade"] == 4, "六维档位逐维取中位数"
    assert agg["overall_evidence"] == "score 82", "证据来自最接近中位数的那份"
    assert [d["key"] for d in agg["source_deductions"]] == ["webpage_url"], "来源扣分按多数票（≥ 半数）"
    assert agg["confidence"] == 0.9
    # 偶数份：中位数取中间两份的平均并四舍五入
    agg2 = aggregate_vote_outputs([_vote_output(80, {"a": 3}), _vote_output(85, {"a": 4})])
    assert agg2["aesthetic_score"] in (82, 83) and agg2["dimensions"]["a"]["grade"] == 4


def test_vote_wrapper_samples_n_times_and_sums_usage(monkeypatch):
    """N 份采样各自走一次供应商调用（关联 ID 各异），聚合成一份节点输出；用量求和，温度只改副本。"""
    import asyncio
    from dataclasses import dataclass
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app import workflow_model_executor as wme
    from app.workflow_model_executor import RealPromptResult, execute_prompt_votes

    @dataclass
    class _Prepared:
        client_config: object

    calls = []
    scores = iter([88, 78, 82, 90])   # 最后一个给 samples=1 的单次调用

    async def fake_execute(prepared, *, client_type=None, on_provider_dispatch=None):
        calls.append(prepared.client_config.temperature)
        if on_provider_dispatch is not None:
            on_provider_dispatch()
        s = next(scores)
        return RealPromptResult(
            node_result={"node_kind": "prompt", "output": _vote_output(s, {"a": 4}), "model_invocation": {"x": 1}},
            invocation_evidence={"raw": s}, request_correlation_id=f"req-{s}", upstream_status_code=200,
            input_tokens=10, output_tokens=5, total_tokens=15, media_usage={}, cache_usage=SimpleNamespace(cached_input_tokens=0, status="none"),
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "execute_prepared_prompt", fake_execute)
    dispatched = []
    prepared = _Prepared(client_config=SimpleNamespace(temperature=0.1, timeout_seconds=30))
    result = asyncio.run(execute_prompt_votes(prepared, samples=3, temperature=0.7, on_provider_dispatch=lambda: dispatched.append(1)))
    assert calls == [0.7, 0.7, 0.7], "采样温度只作用于副本"
    assert prepared.client_config.temperature == 0.1, "冻结配置不改"
    assert dispatched == [1], "供应商发出信号只标一次"
    assert result.node_result["output"]["aesthetic_score"] == 82
    assert result.node_result["model_invocation"]["vote"]["samples"] == 3
    assert result.node_result["model_invocation"]["vote"]["scores"] == [88, 78, 82]
    assert result.request_correlation_id == "req-82", "代表样本是最接近中位数那份"
    assert (result.input_tokens, result.output_tokens, result.total_tokens) == (30, 15, 45)
    assert result.invocation_evidence["vote"]["request_correlation_ids"] == ["req-88", "req-78", "req-82"]
    # 逐样本校准留证：只保留校准读的字段（分数、总评、各维 evaluation/hit_rules），不带 grade 等其它字段
    sample_outputs = result.invocation_evidence["vote"]["sample_outputs"]
    assert [s["aesthetic_score"] for s in sample_outputs] == [88, 78, 82]
    assert all(set(s) == {"aesthetic_score"} for s in sample_outputs)
    assert result.node_result["model_invocation"]["vote"]["sample_outputs"] == sample_outputs

    single = asyncio.run(execute_prompt_votes(_Prepared(client_config=SimpleNamespace(temperature=0.1)), samples=1))
    assert single.request_correlation_id.startswith("req-") and "vote" not in single.invocation_evidence


def test_level_score_bands_and_pairwise_aggregation_prefers_better_level_on_ties():
    from app.workflow_model_executor import aggregate_pairwise_verdicts, level_score_bands

    bands = level_score_bands({"L1": 83, "L2": 70, "L3": 67, "L4": 50, "L5": 0})
    assert bands == {"L1": (83, 100), "L2": (70, 82), "L3": (67, 69), "L4": (50, 66), "L5": (0, 49)}
    # 打败 L3/L4/L5 全部锚点、输给 L1、与 L2 打平 -> L2，段内位置 0.5
    verdicts = [("L1", "worse"), ("L1", "worse"), ("L2", "similar"), ("L2", "similar"),
                ("L3", "better"), ("L4", "better"), ("L5", "better")]
    agg = aggregate_pairwise_verdicts(verdicts, bands)
    assert agg["level"] == "L2" and agg["score"] == 76
    # 全赢 -> L1 顶格；全输 -> L5 底
    assert aggregate_pairwise_verdicts([(lv, "better") for lv in bands], bands)["level"] == "L1"
    assert aggregate_pairwise_verdicts([(lv, "worse") for lv in bands], bands)["level"] == "L5"
    # 与 L1 和 L2 都打平（并列）-> 取更好的 L1（偏召回）
    tie = aggregate_pairwise_verdicts([("L1", "similar"), ("L2", "similar"), ("L3", "better"), ("L4", "better"), ("L5", "better")], bands)
    assert tie["level"] == "L1"


def test_pairwise_wrapper_compares_each_anchor_and_overrides_score(monkeypatch):
    import asyncio
    from dataclasses import replace
    from pathlib import Path
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from app.doubao import ProviderImageSample, _LegacyAutoFidelity
    from app import workflow_model_executor as wme

    def sample(label, role, ordinal):
        return ProviderImageSample(label=label, path=Path("/tmp/x.jpg"), mime_type="image/jpeg", role=role,
                                   ordinal=ordinal, fidelity=_LegacyAutoFidelity(), policy_hash=None)

    anchors = [sample(f"锚点图 {lv}（素材 {i}）", "anchor", i + 1) for i, lv in enumerate(["L1", "L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"])]
    target = sample("待评图片（禁止把锚点图等级直接当作输出）", "target", 0)
    prepared = SimpleNamespace(image_samples=tuple(anchors) + (target,), client_config=SimpleNamespace(timeout_seconds=10),
                               output_budget=300, reasoning_effort=None)

    seen_main = {}
    async def fake_votes(p, *, samples, temperature, client_type, on_provider_dispatch):
        seen_main["samples"] = p.image_samples
        seen_main["serialized"] = p.serialized_provider_request
        return wme.RealPromptResult(
            node_result={"output": {"aesthetic_score": 60, "dimensions": {"a": {"grade": 3}}}, "model_invocation": {"x": 1}},
            invocation_evidence={"e": 1}, request_correlation_id="main", upstream_status_code=200,
            input_tokens=10, output_tokens=5, total_tokens=15, media_usage={}, cache_usage=None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "execute_prompt_votes", fake_votes)
    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "replace", lambda obj, **kw: SimpleNamespace(**{**vars(obj), **kw}) if isinstance(obj, SimpleNamespace) else replace(obj, **kw))
    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "_usage", lambda resp: (2, 1, 3))

    calls = []
    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, cfg):
            pass
    # 锚点标签在比较时被中性化，但要保留原标签用于判定 -> 用 ordinal 反查
    async def chat(self, system_prompt, samples, **kw):
        # 锚点可能在图A 或图B：按 ordinal 找出锚点，给出“待评相对锚点”的真实关系，再按顺序翻成图B相对图A
        anchor = samples[0] if samples[0].ordinal != 0 else samples[1]
        target_first = samples[0].ordinal == 0
        level = [lv for (lv, s) in zip(["L1", "L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"], anchors) if s.ordinal == anchor.ordinal][0]
        calls.append((samples[0].label, samples[1].label, len(samples), target_first))
        truth = "worse" if level == "L1" else "similar" if level == "L2" else "better"
        verdict = {"better": "worse", "worse": "better", "similar": "similar"}[truth] if target_first else truth
        resp = wme.DoubaoResponse.__new__(wme.DoubaoResponse)
        object.__setattr__(resp, "parsed", {"verdict": verdict, "confidence": 0.9, "reason": "r"})
        object.__setattr__(resp, "request_correlation_id", f"pair-{len(calls)}")
        object.__setattr__(resp, "raw_text", "")
        return resp
    FakeClient.chat_json_images = chat

    result = asyncio.run(wme.execute_pairwise_anchors(
        prepared, level_thresholds={"L1": 83, "L2": 70, "L3": 67, "L4": 50, "L5": 0},
        pairwise_samples=1, client_type=FakeClient))
    # 主调用只带每级第一张锚点（5 张）+ 待评图，且不再复用序列化请求
    assert [s.role for s in seen_main["samples"]] == ["anchor"] * 5 + ["target"] and seen_main["serialized"] is None
    # 六张锚点各比较一次，两图一组，标签中性化
    assert len(calls) == 6 and all(c[2] == 2 and {c[0], c[1]} == {"图A", "图B"} for c in calls)
    # 顺序交替：一半待评图在前
    assert sum(1 for c in calls if c[3]) == 3
    assert result.node_result["output"]["aesthetic_score"] == 76 and result.node_result["output"]["dimensions"] == {"a": {"grade": 3}}
    info = result.node_result["model_invocation"]["pairwise"]
    assert info["level"] == "L2" and info["model_score"] == 60 and info["anchors"] == 6
    assert result.total_tokens == 15 + 6 * 3 and result.invocation_evidence["pairwise"]["score"] == 76


def test_vote_failure_keeps_usage_of_every_dispatched_sample(monkeypatch):
    """一路采样失败不能丢掉其余已发出付费请求的用量；发出信号任一路先发都要标（且只标一次）。"""
    import asyncio
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app import workflow_model_executor as wme
    from app.doubao import CacheUsageObservation

    finished = []
    order = iter(range(3))

    async def fake_execute(prepared, *, client_type=None, on_provider_dispatch=None):
        index = next(order)
        if index == 0:
            # 第一路发出前就失败（未发出），不带用量
            raise wme.WorkflowModelExecutorError("model_client_invalid", "发出前失败")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01 * index)
        on_provider_dispatch()
        if index == 1:
            error = wme.WorkflowModelExecutorError("structured_output_schema_mismatch", "schema 不符", retryable=True)
            error.provider_usage = {"input_tokens": 100, "output_tokens": 7, "total_tokens": 107,
                                    "cached_input_tokens": 0, "cache_observation_status": "miss"}
            raise error
        finished.append(index)
        return wme.RealPromptResult(
            node_result={"output": _vote_output(80, {"a": 4})}, invocation_evidence={}, request_correlation_id="ok",
            upstream_status_code=200, input_tokens=200, output_tokens=9, total_tokens=209, media_usage={},
            cache_usage=CacheUsageObservation(status="miss", cached_input_tokens=0, source=None),
            request_usages=({"input_tokens": 200, "output_tokens": 9},), media_request_input_tokens=200)

    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "execute_prepared_prompt", fake_execute)
    dispatched = []
    prepared = SimpleNamespace(client_config=SimpleNamespace(temperature=0.1, timeout_seconds=30))
    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "replace", lambda obj, **kw: SimpleNamespace(**{**vars(obj), **kw}))
    with pytest.raises(wme.WorkflowModelExecutorError) as caught:
        asyncio.run(wme.execute_prompt_votes(prepared, samples=3, on_provider_dispatch=lambda: dispatched.append(1)))
    assert finished == [2], "其余采样要跑完，不能被一路失败打断后丢掉"
    assert dispatched == [1], "第一路未发出，后续发出的采样仍要标记已发出"
    usage = caught.value.provider_usage
    assert (usage["input_tokens"], usage["output_tokens"]) == (300, 16)
    assert usage["request_usages"] == [{"input_tokens": 100, "output_tokens": 7}, {"input_tokens": 200, "output_tokens": 9}]
    assert usage["unknown_usage_request_count"] == 1


def test_pairwise_main_failure_keeps_compare_usage_and_dispatch(monkeypatch):
    """主调用失败但比较请求已发出：用量合计带到异常上，发出信号必须已触发（否则会被记成未发出 rejected）。"""
    import asyncio
    from dataclasses import replace
    from pathlib import Path
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from app.doubao import ProviderImageSample, _LegacyAutoFidelity
    from app import workflow_model_executor as wme

    def sample(label, role, ordinal):
        return ProviderImageSample(label=label, path=Path("/tmp/x.jpg"), mime_type="image/jpeg", role=role,
                                   ordinal=ordinal, fidelity=_LegacyAutoFidelity(), policy_hash=None)

    anchors = [sample(f"锚点图 {lv}（素材 {i}）", "anchor", i + 1) for i, lv in enumerate(["L1", "L3"])]
    target = sample("待评图片", "target", 0)
    prepared = SimpleNamespace(image_samples=tuple(anchors) + (target,), client_config=SimpleNamespace(timeout_seconds=10),
                               output_budget=300, reasoning_effort=None)

    async def failing_votes(p, *, samples, temperature, client_type, on_provider_dispatch):
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
        raise wme.WorkflowModelExecutorError("model_client_invalid", "主调用发出前失败")

    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "execute_prompt_votes", failing_votes)
    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "replace", lambda obj, **kw: SimpleNamespace(**{**vars(obj), **kw}) if isinstance(obj, SimpleNamespace) else replace(obj, **kw))
    monkeypatch.setattr(wme, "_usage", lambda resp: (40, 3, 43))

    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, cfg):
            self.hook = None

        def set_dispatch_hook(self, hook):
            self.hook = hook

        async def chat_json_images(self, system_prompt, samples, **kw):
            self.hook()
            resp = wme.DoubaoResponse.__new__(wme.DoubaoResponse)
            object.__setattr__(resp, "parsed", {"verdict": "similar", "confidence": 0.9, "reason": "r"})
            object.__setattr__(resp, "request_correlation_id", "pair")
            object.__setattr__(resp, "raw_text", "")
            return resp

    dispatched = []
    with pytest.raises(wme.WorkflowModelExecutorError) as caught:
        asyncio.run(wme.execute_pairwise_anchors(
            prepared, level_thresholds={"L1": 83, "L2": 70, "L3": 67, "L4": 50, "L5": 0},
            client_type=FakeClient, on_provider_dispatch=lambda: dispatched.append(1)))
    assert caught.value.code == "model_client_invalid"
    assert dispatched == [1]
    usage = caught.value.provider_usage
    assert (usage["input_tokens"], usage["output_tokens"]) == (80, 6)
    assert len(usage["request_usages"]) == 2 and usage["unknown_usage_request_count"] == 1


def test_schema_re_ask_key_is_the_steps_own_identity_on_every_route():
    """在线路线（无传输策略）下 schema 重问键必须被接受；批量路线下重问的续调用键也要被接受；外来键仍拒绝。"""
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix, policy, VERSION, BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS, DEADLINE_SECONDS
    from app.workflow_model_executor import SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX, invocation_identity_verified

    online = {"inference_route": {"mode": "online"}}
    assert policy(online) is None
    assert invocation_identity_verified("root", transport_mode=None, runtime=online)
    assert invocation_identity_verified(SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX + "root", transport_mode=None, runtime=online)
    assert not invocation_identity_verified("foreach:1", transport_mode=None, runtime=online)
    assert not invocation_identity_verified(invocation_prefix("root") + "online:1", transport_mode="online", runtime=online)

    batch = {"inference_route": {"mode": "batch", "fallback_policy": {
        "version": VERSION, "batch_max_attempts": BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS, "deadline_seconds": DEADLINE_SECONDS}}}
    assert policy(batch) is not None
    assert invocation_identity_verified(invocation_prefix("root") + "batch:1", transport_mode="batch", runtime=batch)
    assert invocation_identity_verified(invocation_prefix(SCHEMA_RETRY_PREFIX + "root") + "online:2:reservation:3", transport_mode="online", runtime=batch)
    assert not invocation_identity_verified(invocation_prefix("root") + "batch:1", transport_mode=None, runtime=batch)
    assert not invocation_identity_verified(invocation_prefix("other") + "batch:1", transport_mode="batch", runtime=batch)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_node_executors.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest


def _node(kind: str = "prompt") -> dict:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": "prompt_c_instance",
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": "提示词 C",
        "config": {
            "asset_key": "prompt_c",
            "prompt_version_id": 17,
            "model_registry_id": 5,
            "max_tokens": 4096,
            "thinking_level": "medium",
            "component_snapshot": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "version_id": 17,
                "content_hash": "a" * 64,
            },
            "model_snapshot": {
                "model_registry_id": 5,
                "model_id": "fixture-model",
                "provider": "fixture",
                "runtime_config": {"max_tokens": 8192},
            },
        },
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id"],
    }

def test_rule_executor_registry_covers_all_operator_module_types() -> None:
    """Adding or removing a module type must not silently fall back to noop."""

    from app.evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS, OPTIONAL_MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
    from app.workflow_node_executors import registered_rule_module_types

    # 必备切片 + 可选模块类型（字典撞库、分类定级表）都必须注册执行器，缺一个就会静默退化成 noop
    assert registered_rule_module_types() == set(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS) | set(OPTIONAL_MODULE_TYPE_KEYS)

def test_score_level_matcher_executes_frozen_case_score_formula_without_delete() -> None:
    """Case filtering must be deterministic and may only recommend deletion."""

    from app.workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutionContext,
        execute_native_node,
    )

    node = _node("rule_module")
    node["node_instance_id"] = "case_final_score"
    node["config"] = {
        "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
        "rule_module_version_id": 42,
        "component_snapshot": {
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "version_id": 42,
            "version": "1",
            "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
            "content_hash": "c" * 64,
            "content_json": """
            {
              "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
              "levels": [
                {"level":"L1","enabled":true,"min_score":80},
                {"level":"L2","enabled":true,"min_score":60},
                {"level":"L3","enabled":true,"min_score":40},
                {"level":"L4","enabled":true,"min_score":20},
                {"level":"L5","enabled":true,"min_score":0}
              ],
              "case_score_formula": {
                "schema_version": "case-final-score-v1",
                "model_score_field": "score",
                "quality_score_field": "quality_score",
                "time_score_field": "time_score",
                "threshold_field": "filter_threshold",
                "comparison": "strict_gt",
                "deletion_mode": "recommend_only"
              }
            }
            """,
        },
    }

    result = execute_native_node(
        NodeExecutionContext(
            run_snapshot={
                "workflow": {"manifest": {"schema_version": "workflow-v3"}},
                "runtime_context": {
                    "input_manifest": {
                        # Deliberately conflict with the immutable intake facts.
                        # Golden truth must never participate in scoring.
                        "expected_result": {
                            "quality_score": 900,
                            "time_score": 800,
                            "filter_threshold": 1,
                        },
                        "case": {
                            "quality_score": 10,
                            "time_score": 5,
                            "filter_threshold": 84,
                        }
                    }
                },
            },
            node=node,
            input_manifest={
                "upstream": {
                    "case_text_prompt": {
                        "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                        "node_instance_id": "case_text_prompt",
                        "node_kind": "prompt",
                        "status": "succeeded",
                        "output": {"score": 70, "reason": "fixture"},
                    },
                },
            },
            raw_fixture_output={},
            attempt_no=1,
            started_at=None,
        )
    )

    assert result["output"] == {
        "score": 85.0,
        "level": "L1",
        "filter_status": "pass",
        "reason": "final_score 85.0 > filter_threshold 84.0",
        "model_score": 70.0,
        "quality_score": 10.0,
        "time_score": 5.0,
        "final_score": 85.0,
        "filter_threshold": 84.0,
        "filter_result": 1,
        "delete_recommendation": False,
        "recommended_image_ids": [],
        "case_review": {"score": 70, "reason": "fixture"},
        "side_effect_executed": False,
        "deletion_mode": "recommend_only",
    }

def test_case_score_never_falls_back_to_expected_truth_for_model_score() -> None:

    )

        "component_snapshot": {
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "version_id": 42,
            "version": "1",
            "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
            "content_hash": "c" * 64,
            "content_json": """
            {
              "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
              "levels": [
                {"level":"L1","enabled":true,"min_score":80},
                {"level":"L2","enabled":true,"min_score":60},
                {"level":"L3","enabled":true,"min_score":40},
                {"level":"L4","enabled":true,"min_score":20},
                {"level":"L5","enabled":true,"min_score":0}

                "schema_version": "case-final-score-v1",
                "model_score_field": "score",
                "quality_score_field": "quality_score",
                "time_score_field": "time_score",

def test_native_prompt_result_freezes_component_model_and_upstream_evidence() -> None:
    """Changing current assets must not change a node's frozen result identity."""

    from app.workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutionContext,
        execute_native_node,
    )

    node = _node()
    context = NodeExecutionContext(
        run_snapshot={
            "workflow": {
                "manifest": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "nodes": [node],
                    "edges": [],
                }
            },
            "runtime_context": {"input_manifest": {"content_key": "asset-1"}},
        },
        node=node,
        input_manifest={
            "upstream": {
                "upstream": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                    "output": {"content_key": "asset-1", "request_id": "req-1"},
                }
            }
        },
        raw_fixture_output={"fixture": "noop"},
        attempt_no=1,
        started_at=datetime(2026, 8, 26, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    result = execute_native_node(context)

    assert result["schema_version"] == "workflow-node-result-v1"
    assert result["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c_instance"
    assert result["node_kind"] == "prompt"
    assert result["status"] == "succeeded"
    assert result["upstream_refs"] == ["upstream"]
    assert result["component_version_id"] == 17
    assert result["component_hash"] == "a" * 64
    assert result["model_snapshot"] == node["config"]["model_snapshot"]
    assert result["output"] == {
        "fixture": "deterministic_prompt",
        "input_hash": result["input_fingerprint"],
    }
    assert result["fixture"] == {
        "key": "prompt.default.v1",
        "hash": "39fd0cd0392455d66909ac928404d50610f1c486ab903d2a8c1a09ab2a6f5232",
    }
    assert result["evidence"] == {"request_id": "req-1"}
    assert result["attempt"] == 1
    assert result["started_at"] == "2026-08-26T00:00:00+00:00"
    assert result["finished_at"]

def test_executability_gate_rejects_unknown_executor_and_code_fields() -> None:
    """A manifest must not select an implementation or carry executable text."""

    from app.workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutorError,
        assert_native_workflow_executable,
    )

    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "nodes": [_node("custom_python")],
        "edges": [],
    }
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as missing:
        assert_native_workflow_executable(manifest)
    assert missing.value.code == "workflow_executor_missing"

    unsafe = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "nodes": [_node()],
        "edges": [],
        "runtime": {"sql": "select * from secrets"},
    }
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as forbidden:
        assert_native_workflow_executable(unsafe)
    assert forbidden.value.code == "arbitrary_code_field_forbidden"


def test_deterministic_schema_value_honours_const_and_min_length() -> None:
    from app.workflow_node_executors import (
        _deterministic_schema_value,
        _validate_output_value,
    )

    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "required": ["contract_version", "evidence", "score"],
        "properties": {
            "contract_version": {"type": "string", "const": "frozen-v3"},
            "evidence": {"type": "string", "minLength": 3},
            "score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100},
        },
    }
    value = _deterministic_schema_value(schema)
    assert value["contract_version"] == "frozen-v3"
    assert len(value["evidence"]) >= 3
    _validate_output_value(value, schema)
