===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_runtime_recovery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import importlib.util
import asyncio
import json
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ModelConfig,
    ModelQuotaPool,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    QueueSchedulerState,
    ScriptVersion,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
)
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
    recover_expired_runtime_steps,
)
from tests.test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow
from app import worker


def _fixture_module():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec("app.workflow_fixture_executor") is not None
    return importlib.import_module("app.workflow_fixture_executor")


def test_process_runtime_step_once_retries_fixture_failure(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'fixture-runtime.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            script = db.scalar(select(ScriptVersion))
            assert script is not None
            script.manifest_json = json.dumps({"fixture": "fail_once"})
            db.commit()
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="fixture-run",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
                    owner="platform",
                    reason="fixture",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))
        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is True

        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert [attempt.status for attempt in attempts] == [
                "failed",
                "retryable",
            ]
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == "retryable"

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is True

        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert attempts[-1].status == "succeeded"
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_preflight_failure_evidence_is_persisted_without_an_invocation(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'preflight-evidence.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="preflight-evidence",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={
                        "queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"
                    },
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "3d:preflight"},
                    owner="platform",
                    reason="fixture",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))

        def fail_before_dispatch(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise fixture.FixtureExecutionError(
                "vision_request_body_too_large",
                "模型请求体超过安全上限",
                retryable=False,
                evidence={
                    "serialized_request_body_bytes": 501,
                    "operational_max_bytes": 500,
                },
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "execute_fixture", fail_before_dispatch)

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("preflight-worker") is True

        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            checkpoint = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)
            assert checkpoint["status"] == "failed_preflight"
            assert checkpoint["failure"] == {
                "code": "vision_request_body_too_large",
                "evidence": {
                    "serialized_request_body_bytes": 501,
                    "operational_max_bytes": 500,
                },
            }
            assert attempt.checkpoint_hash == fixture.hash_manifest(checkpoint)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_complete_runtime_step_retries_mysql_deadlock_in_a_fresh_transaction(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    events: list[str] = []
    completion_attempts = 0

    class MysqlDeadlock(RuntimeError):
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            super().__init__(1213, "Deadlock found when trying to get lock")

    class FakeSession:
        def commit(self) -> None:
            events.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            events.append("rollback")

        def close(self) -> None:
            events.append("close")

    def complete(_db, _attempt_id, _lease_token, _output_manifest) -> None:
        nonlocal completion_attempts
        completion_attempts += 1
        events.append(f"complete:{completion_attempts}")
        if completion_attempts == 1:
            raise MysqlDeadlock()

    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", FakeSession)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "complete_runtime_step", complete)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        fixture.time,
        "sleep",
        lambda seconds: events.append(f"sleep:{seconds}"),
    )

    fixture._complete_runtime_step_with_retry(
        attempt_id=42,
        lease_token="lease-token",
        output_manifest={"ok": True},
    )

    assert completion_attempts == 2
    assert events == [
        "complete:1",
        "rollback",
        "close",
        "sleep:0.05",
        "complete:2",
        "commit",
        "close",
    ]


def test_expired_foreach_parent_terminalizes_every_reserved_child_call(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach-recovery.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="foreach-parent-recovery",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "case:1"},
                    owner="platform",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            claimed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=1)
            attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "foreach-worker",
                lease_seconds=1,
                now=claimed_at,
            )
            assert attempt_id is not None
            for key in ("item:a:attempt:1", "item:b:attempt:1"):
                db.add(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation(
                        production_run_id=run.id,
                        step_attempt_id=attempt_id,
                        invocation_key=key,
                        node_instance_id="root",
                        status="reserved",
                        provider="doubao",
                        model_id="seed",
                        evidence_json="{}",
                    )
                )
            db.flush()

            assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(
                db,
                now=claimed_at + timedelta(seconds=2),
            ) == 1

            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key
                )
            ).all()
            assert [item.status for item in invocations] == ["failed", "failed"]
            assert {
                item.error_code for item in invocations
            } == {"model_invocation_outcome_unknown"}
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status == "failed"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_worker_process_one_prioritizes_runtime_step(monkeypatch) -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []
    event_loop_thread = threading.get_ident()

    def recover() -> int:
        assert threading.get_ident() != event_loop_thread
        calls.append("recover")
        return 0

    def process_runtime(_worker_id: str) -> bool:
        assert threading.get_ident() != event_loop_thread
        calls.append("runtime")
        return True

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "recover_runtime_once",
        recover,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "process_workflow_runtime_step_once",
        process_runtime,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "claim_next_job",
        lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(AssertionError("legacy job claimed")),
    )

    assert asyncio.run(worker.process_one()) is True
    assert calls == ["recover", "runtime"]


def test_evaluation_worker_group_skips_duplicate_recovery_scan(monkeypatch) -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "recover_runtime_once",
        lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
            AssertionError("evaluation worker group must not run recovery")
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "process_workflow_runtime_step_once",
        lambda _worker_id: calls.append("runtime") or False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "claim_next_job", lambda: None)

    assert asyncio.run(worker.process_one(run_recovery=False)) is False
    assert calls == ["runtime"]


def test_runtime_worker_reuses_persisted_global_limit(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-limit.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                QueueSchedulerState(
                    id=1,
                    policy_version="queue-policy-v1",
                    global_limit=4,
                )
            )
            db.commit()
        captured: dict[str, int] = {}
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "claim_next_runtime_step",
            lambda _db, _worker_id, *, global_limit: captured.update(
                global_limit=global_limit
            ) or None,
        )

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is False
        assert captured["global_limit"] == 4
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    tmp_path,

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-quota-pools.db'}")

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first_pool = ModelQuotaPool(
                pool_key="doubao-account-a",
                name="豆包独立额度 A",
                provider="doubao",
                scope_kind="account",
                scope_identifier="account-a",
                max_concurrency=80,
                lease_seconds=180,
                enabled=True,
                created_by="test",
            )
            second_pool = ModelQuotaPool(
                pool_key="doubao-account-b",
                name="豆包独立额度 B",
                provider="doubao",
                scope_kind="account",
                scope_identifier="account-b",
                max_concurrency=110,
                lease_seconds=180,
                enabled=True,
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add_all([first_pool, second_pool])
            db.flush()
            db.add_all(
                [
                    ModelConfig(
                        name="doubao-model-a",
                        provider="doubao",
                        encrypted_api_key="encrypted-test-key-a",
                        max_concurrency=10,
                        quota_pool_id=first_pool.id,
                        active=True,
                    ),
                    ModelConfig(
                        name="doubao-model-b",
                        provider="doubao",
                        encrypted_api_key="encrypted-test-key-b",
                        max_concurrency=10,
                        quota_pool_id=second_pool.id,
                        active=True,
                    ),
                ]
            )
            db.commit()
        captured: dict[str, int] = {}
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "claim_next_runtime_step",
            lambda _db, _worker_id, *, global_limit: captured.update(
                global_limit=global_limit
            )
            or None,
        )

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is False
        assert captured["global_limit"] == 190
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_failure_report_retries_mysql_deadlock_in_a_fresh_transaction(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    # 失败回报与成功回报对称：fail_runtime_step 撞上 MySQL 死锁时必须换新事务重试，
    # 否则整段回报丢失、步骤停在 running 直到租约到期被回收器改写成 STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED。
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    events: list[str] = []
    report_attempts = 0

    class MysqlDeadlock(RuntimeError):
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            super().__init__(1213, "Deadlock found when trying to get lock")

    class FakeSession:
        def get(self, _model, _key):
            return None

        def commit(self) -> None:
            events.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            events.append("rollback")

        def close(self) -> None:
            events.append("close")

    def fail(_db, _attempt_id, _lease_token, code, _message, **_kwargs) -> None:
        nonlocal report_attempts
        report_attempts += 1
        events.append(f"fail:{report_attempts}:{code}")
        if report_attempts == 1:
            raise MysqlDeadlock()

    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", FakeSession)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "fail_runtime_step", fail)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        fixture.time,
        "sleep",
        lambda seconds: events.append(f"sleep:{seconds}"),
    )

    fixture._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(
        attempt_id=42,
        lease_token="lease-token",
        exc=fixture.FixtureExecutionError(
            "model_provider_transient_parse",
            "模型服务暂时不可用，可按工作流重试策略恢复",
            error_policy_kind="fail",
            retryable=True,
        ),
    )

    assert report_attempts == 2
    assert events == [
        "fail:1:model_provider_transient_parse",
        "rollback",
        "close",
        "sleep:0.05",
        "fail:2:model_provider_transient_parse",
        "commit",
        "close",
    ]


def test_failure_report_does_not_retry_non_transient_errors(monkeypatch) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    calls = 0

    class FakeSession:
        def get(self, _model, _key):
            return None

        def commit(self) -> None:
            pass

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            pass

        def close(self) -> None:
            pass

    def fail(*_args, **_kwargs) -> None:
        nonlocal calls
        calls += 1
        raise ValueError("boom")

    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", FakeSession)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "fail_runtime_step", fail)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        fixture._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(
            attempt_id=7,
            lease_token="t",
            exc=fixture.FixtureExecutionError(
                "x", "y", error_policy_kind="fail", retryable=True
            ),
        )
    assert calls == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_short_circuit.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.workflow_short_circuit import (
    ShortCircuitConflict,
    ShortCircuitError,
    ShortCircuitRule,
    resolve_short_circuit,
    validate_short_circuit_rules,
)


LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")


def _rule(
    target_level: str = "L2",
    priority: int = 20,
    *,
    rule_key: str = "severe-blur",
    when: dict | None = None,
) -> ShortCircuitRule:
    return ShortCircuitRule(
        rule_key=rule_key,
        when=when or {"path": "quality.blur", "op": "eq", "value": "severe"},
        target_level=target_level,
        priority=priority,
        skip_roles=("grading", "level_matching"),
        rule_version="short-circuit-rule-v1",
    )


def test_any_frozen_level_can_finalize() -> None:
    decision = resolve_short_circuit(
        rules=[_rule(target_level="L2")],
        facts={"quality": {"blur": "severe"}},
        valid_levels=LEVELS,
    )

    assert decision is not None
    assert decision.target_level == "L2"
    assert decision.rule_key == "severe-blur"
    assert decision.evidence["matched_conditions"] == [
        {
            "path": "quality.blur",
            "op": "eq",
            "expected": "severe",
            "actual": "severe",
        }
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("when", "facts"),
    [
        (
            {"path": "quality.blur", "op": "in", "value": ["mild", "severe"]},
            {"quality": {"blur": "severe"}},
        ),
        ({"path": "quality.blur", "op": "exists", "value": True}, {"quality": {"blur": "severe"}}),
        ({"path": "quality.score", "op": "gte", "value": 80}, {"quality": {"score": 80}}),
        ({"path": "quality.score", "op": "lte", "value": 20}, {"quality": {"score": 19}}),
        (
            {
                "all": [
                    {"path": "quality.blur", "op": "eq", "value": "severe"},
                    {
                        "any": [
                            {"path": "quality.score", "op": "lte", "value": 20},
                            {"path": "quality.blocked", "op": "eq", "value": True},
                        ]
                    },
                ]
            },
            {"quality": {"blur": "severe", "score": 19}},
        ),
    ],
)
def test_condition_operators_are_evaluated_against_frozen_facts(
    when: dict,
    facts: dict,
) -> None:
    decision = resolve_short_circuit(
        rules=[_rule(when=when)],
        facts=facts,
        valid_levels=LEVELS,
    )
    assert decision is not None


def test_equal_priority_conflict_fails_closed() -> None:
    facts = {"quality": {"blur": "severe"}}
    with pytest.raises(ShortCircuitConflict, match="人工复核") as exc_info:
        resolve_short_circuit(
            [
                _rule("L2", 20, rule_key="alpha"),
                _rule("L4", 20, rule_key="beta"),
            ],
            facts,
            LEVELS,
        )
    assert exc_info.value.code == "short_circuit_conflict"


def test_equal_priority_same_level_is_stable_by_rule_key() -> None:
    decision = resolve_short_circuit(
        [
            _rule("L2", 20, rule_key="z-last"),
            _rule("L2", 20, rule_key="a-first"),
        ],
        {"quality": {"blur": "severe"}},
        LEVELS,
    )
    assert decision is not None
    assert decision.rule_key == "a-first"
    assert decision.evidence["matched_rule_keys"] == ["a-first", "z-last"]


def test_disabled_category_level_fails_closed() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ShortCircuitError, match="冻结等级") as exc_info:
        resolve_short_circuit(
            [_rule("L5")],
            {"quality": {"blur": "severe"}},
            ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"),
        )
    assert exc_info.value.code == "short_circuit_target_level_invalid"


def test_no_matching_rule_returns_none() -> None:
    assert (
        resolve_short_circuit(
            [_rule()],
            {"quality": {"blur": "none"}},
            LEVELS,
        )
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("actual", "operator", "expected"),
    [
        (True, "eq", 1),
        (False, "eq", 0),
        (True, "in", [1, "true"]),
        (False, "in", [0, "false"]),
    ],
)
def test_boolean_facts_never_match_numeric_short_circuit_values(
    actual: bool,
    operator: str,
    expected: object,
) -> None:
    assert (
        resolve_short_circuit(
            [
                _rule(
                    when={
                        "path": "quality.blocked",
                        "op": operator,
                        "value": expected,
                    }
                )
            ],
            {"quality": {"blocked": actual}},
            LEVELS,
        )
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "when",
    [
        {"path": "quality.labels", "op": "in", "value": "L4"},
        {"path": "quality.score", "op": "gte", "value": "80"},
        {"path": "quality.score", "op": "lte", "value": True},
        {
            "all": [
                {"path": "quality.score", "op": "gte", "value": 80},
                {"path": "quality.labels", "op": "in", "value": "L4"},
            ]
        },
    ],
)
def test_publication_rejects_invalid_operands_without_runtime_facts(
    when: dict,
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ShortCircuitError) as exc_info:
        validate_short_circuit_rules([_rule(when=when)], LEVELS)

    assert exc_info.value.code == "short_circuit_condition_invalid"


def test_numeric_short_circuit_accepts_arbitrarily_large_json_integers() -> None:
    huge_integer = 10**400
    decision = resolve_short_circuit(
        [
            _rule(
                when={
                    "path": "quality.counter",
                    "op": "gte",
                    "value": huge_integer,
                }
            )
        ],
        {"quality": {"counter": huge_integer}},
        LEVELS,
    )

    assert decision is not None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_short_circuit_levels.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.workflow_short_circuit import (
    ShortCircuitConflict,
    downstream_skipped_nodes,
    resolve_short_circuit,
    validate_short_circuit_rules,
)


LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")


def _rule(target_level: str, *, priority: int = 50) -> dict:
    return {
        "rule_version": "short-circuit-rule-v1",
        "rule_key": f"direct-{target_level.lower()}",
        "when": {
            "all": [
                {"path": "output.hit", "op": "eq", "value": True},
                {
                    "any": [
                        {"path": "output.confidence", "op": "gte", "value": 0.9},
                        {"path": "output.force", "op": "eq", "value": True},
                    ]
                },
            ]
        },
        "target_level": target_level,
        "priority": priority,
        "skip_roles": ["grading", "level_matching"],
        "skip_nodes": ["special_expensive_node"],
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target_level", LEVELS)
def test_every_enabled_level_can_be_an_explicit_short_circuit_target(
    target_level: str,
) -> None:
    decision = resolve_short_circuit(
        [_rule(target_level)],
        {"output": {"hit": True, "confidence": 0.95}},
        LEVELS,
    )

    assert decision is not None
    assert decision.target_level == target_level
    assert decision.evidence["skip_roles"] == ["grading", "level_matching"]
    assert decision.evidence["skip_nodes"] == ["special_expensive_node"]


def test_explicit_nodes_and_roles_skip_only_descendants_and_never_protected_nodes() -> None:
    manifest = {
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_instance_id": "single_prompt",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "config": {"execution_role": "classification"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "ordinary_grading",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "config": {"execution_role": "grading"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "special_expensive_node",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {"execution_role": "reconcile"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "audit_record",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {"execution_role": "audit"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "result_projection",
                "kind": "downstream_projection",
                "config": {"execution_role": "grading"},
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "unrelated_grading",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "config": {"execution_role": "grading"},
            },
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"from": "single_prompt", "to": "ordinary_grading"},
            {"from": "ordinary_grading", "to": "special_expensive_node"},
            {"from": "special_expensive_node", "to": "audit_record"},
            {"from": "audit_record", "to": "result_projection"},
        ],
    }

    skipped = downstream_skipped_nodes(
        manifest,
        source_node_id="single_prompt",
        skip_roles=["grading", "audit"],
        skip_nodes=[
            "special_expensive_node",
            "audit_record",
            "result_projection",
            "unrelated_grading",
        ],
    )

    assert skipped == ("ordinary_grading", "special_expensive_node")


def test_short_circuit_rejects_malformed_explicit_node_targets() -> None:
    rule = _rule("L4")
    rule["skip_nodes"] = ["valid", ""]

    with pytest.raises(Exception) as exc_info:
        validate_short_circuit_rules([rule], LEVELS)

    assert getattr(exc_info.value, "code", None) == "short_circuit_skip_nodes_invalid"


def test_equal_priority_different_levels_still_fails_closed() -> None:
    first = _rule("L3")
    second = _rule("L4")
    second["rule_key"] = "direct-l4-conflict"

    with pytest.raises(ShortCircuitConflict) as exc_info:
        resolve_short_circuit(
            [first, second],
            {"output": {"hit": True, "force": True}},
            LEVELS,
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "short_circuit_conflict"
